- Answer from this content only. If it is not covered, say so; never guess numbers.
- Find relevant pages in index.md by title, aliases (aka) and categories.

- Cite the [[Page]] each fact comes from. Reply in the user's language.

# Scorpius
Aliases: Candidate Scorpius, Mayor Scorpius
Categories: Non-player characters, Mayor, Events, Mayor Election
[Scorpius Scorpius]
Also known as: Mayor Scorpius
Type: Mayor ([[Mayor Election]])
[Locations]
Description: Appears here when elected as Mayor ([[Mayor Election]]).
Location: [[Community Center]]
Coordinates: 6.5 79 19.5
Description: Appears here when elected as Mayor ([[Mayor Election]]). Also appears on an outer stand when running to become Mayor.
Location: [[Election Room]]
Coordinates: -3.5 49 34.5
Description: Appears here when elected as Mayor ([[Mayor Election]]).
Location: [[Dark Auction]]
Coordinates: 87.5 55 152.5
Tooltip: Year # Candidate; Votes: # (#%); Last elected: #y ago; __________________________ Bribe If Scorpius wins and you voted, for him, Mayor Scorpius will, offer you &#60;#bribe amount&#62; coins, as a token of gratitude.; Darker Auctions Scorpius will intrude in Dark Auctions, increasing the amount of rounds to 7 and offering special items.; __________________________; Click to vote for Scorpius!
Scorpius is a mayoral candidate that can be elected during the [[Mayor Election]] event. His perks focus around the [[Dark Auction]]. He is one of the five special candidates to run for election.
## Mayoral Perks
Candidate Scorpius is a special candidate, and will run for election according to the [[Mayor Election|Special Candidates Election Cycle]].
  [[Scorpius]] Scorpius
1. Bribe: If Scorpius wins and you voted for him, Mayor Scorpius will offer you 50,000 [[Coins]] - 1,000,000 [[Coins]] as a token of gratitude.
2. Darker Auctions: Scorpius will intrude in [[Dark Auction|Dark Auctions]], increasing the number of rounds to 7 and offering special items.
### Bribe
If Scorpius wins and the user voted for him, the user will be eligible to claim the bribe reward by speaking to Scorpius while elected. He will reward the user coins, based on the amount of playtime they have on their current profile when they redeem the bribe. For example, if the user voted for Scorpius with 295 hours of playtime at the time of voting, but 305 hours at the time of claiming the bribe, they will receive 1,000,000 [[Coins]], not 500,000 [[Coins]].
|Playtime|Bribe Amount|
|-|-|
|0 - 50 hours|50,000 [[Coins]]|
|50 - 100 hours|100,000 [[Coins]]|
|100 - 150 hours|250,000 [[Coins]]|
|150 - 300 hours|500,000 [[Coins]]|
|300+ hours|1,000,000 [[Coins]]|
### Darker Auctions
When Scorpius is elected mayor and his Darker Auctions perk is available, there will be 8 items auctioned instead of 5. The auctioned items will be randomly selected from the possible items available from that round.
Each item may only appear once: items that appear cannot appear in any following rounds.

# Scout Scardius
Aliases: Scardius
Categories: Non-player characters
[Scout Scardius]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Talks about how to damage [[Glacite Maniacs]].
Location: [[Dwarven Base Camp]]
Coordinates: -16.5 121 232.5
Scout Scardius is an [[NPC]] within the [[Dwarven Base Camp]]. The player can talk to them to learn how to attack [[Glacite Maniacs]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[NPC] Scardius: Scout or not, I'm never leaving the safety of the Campfire again! [NPC] Scardius: When I started mining in the tunnels, I fell deep down into a Glacite Mineshaft. [NPC] Scardius: Down there, I got attacked by all sorts of creatures, and my sword did like no damage to them! [NPC] Scardius: I thought I was done for, so I pulled out my pickaxe and started swinging. [NPC] Scardius: It actually seemed to be quite effective! [NPC] Scardius: So, if you're ever in the Glacite Mineshafts and get attacked, try using your pickaxe or drill as a weapon! [NPC] Scardius: The higher your Ⓟ Breaking Power, the more damage it seems to do to those creatures...|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Scout Scardius: Do you have any pumpkins I can wear as a mask while down in the Glacite Tunnels?|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Scout Scardius: Thanks, hopefully I'll be safe with this to hide behind!|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Scout Scardius: You want me to go back down there with nothing to hide behind? Bro...|

# Shania
Categories: Non-player characters, Farming, Quests
[Shania]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: The cow of [[Farmer Rigby]].
Location: [[Farm]]
Coordinates: 59.5 72 -144.5
Shania is an [[NPC]] cow that is owned by [[Farmer Rigby]].
## Quest
Shania is a part of the [[Quests|First Harvest]] quest. [[Farmer Rigby]] asks the player to collect 10 [[Wheat]] to feed their cow, Shania. Upon giving the [[Wheat]], the player will be rewarded with +20 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[NPC] Shania: Moo.|
|None|[NPC] Shania: Moo!|
|None|[NPC] Shania: Moooooo!|
## Trivia
- Once per week, Shania can be left-clicked with an empty bucket to turn it into a [[Magical Milk Bucket]].
- Right-clicking Shania with an empty bucket will give the player a regular Milk Bucket.

# Shania (Rift)
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Shania]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Requests various items obtained with the [[Wand of Farming]], rewarding the [[Proto-Chicken]].
Location: [[Dreadfarm]]
Coordinates: -47.5 72 -161
Shania is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Dreadfarm]]. They give the player [[Wand of Farming]] and provide an explanation to how to collect the three collections available in Dreadfarm.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First interaction|[NPC] Shania: Moo! [NPC] Shania: The farmer is sick for a totally unknown reason. [NPC] Shania: None of the animals here have eaten since they started existing. [NPC] Shania: I would become a farmer mooself, but I don't have opposable thumbs. [NPC] Shania: You'll have to do the work. Take this Wand of Farming!|
|Before giving requested items|[NPC] Shania: We will need a lot of stockpiles to survive through an eternity. [NPC] Shania: Please bring me: [NPC] Shania: 2 Caducous Stem Bunch [NPC] Shania: 2 Wilted Berberis Bunch [NPC] Shania: 2 Agaricus Cap Bunch Select an option: ➜ [How do I harvest Caducous?] ➜ [How do I harvest Berberis?] ➜ [How do I harvest Agaricus?]|
|Selecting [How do I harvest Caducous?]|[NPC] Shania: Caducous are upside-down plants which grow near humid environmengs. [NPC] Shania: Simply collect them from top to bottom!|
|Selecting [How do I harvest Berberis?]|[NPC] Shania: There was once a powerful necromancer who lived in the Catacombs. [NPC] Shania: After a falling out with its administration, she retired to the Rift. [NPC] Shania: She started growing Wilted Berberis in the farm plots and operated the Dreadfarm Bed & Breakfast. [NPC] Shania: Sadly, she passed when her brain melted in an unrelated incident. [NPC] Shania: Anyway, you can harvest the farm patches around the remnants of her decrepit lifework.|
|Selecting [How do I harvest Agaricus?]|[NPC] Shania: If you look at the mushrooms around the Dreadfarm... [NPC] Shania: ... You look intently... [NPC] Shania: ... and then POP! [NPC] Shania: Bada bing, bada boom, you click it when it's red!|
|Giving the requested items|[NPC] Shania: I can personally attest, all of this food is very good. [NPC] Shania: You've done eggcellent work, [player name]. [NPC] Shania: I want you to have this Proto-Chicken. [NPC] Shania: Time is a bit muddy here. I found it when I first spawned in this farm.|
|Without owning a [[Proto-Chicken]] or [[Chicken N Egg Timecharm]] after giving the items|[NPC] Shania: Did you lose the first chicken ever? [NPC] Shania: Happens. Here's another one.|
|If the requested items have been given|[NPC] Shania: I'd love to moove around, but I'm stuck here helping the farmer.|
|If the requested items have been given|[NPC] Shania: As a cow, all I want to do is eat, sleep and moo.|
|If the requested items have been given|[NPC] Shania: Sometimes I sleep and dream about touching grass, what about you?|

# Shepherd
Categories: Non-player characters
[Shepherd]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Tasks the player with shearing all of his sheep at once.
Location: [[Shepherd's Keep]]
Coordinates: 388.5 85 -372.5
The Shepherd is an [[NPC]] located in the house in the [[Shepherd's Keep]] in the [[Mushroom Desert]]. He gives the player the task of shearing all his sheep at once. After having all the [[Sheep]] sheared at once, the Shepherd will reward the player with 4 [[Pelts]].
## Dialogue
- Grammar and/or punctuation mistakes are intentional, as they appear so in the dialogue in-game.
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Shepherd: Hello traveler! [NPC] Shepherd: Welcome to my keep, my sheep are some of the best in the world. [NPC] Shepherd: They have regenerative properties that allow for them to regenerate their wool faster than normal! [NPC] Shepherd: I'm feeling tired today. [NPC] Shepherd: Could you shear all my sheep at once? [NPC] Shepherd: I will have a reward if you do so!|
|Not all [[Sheep]] Sheared|[NPC] Shepherd: You didn't shear enough sheep!|
|All [[Sheep]] Sheared|[NPC] Shepherd: Thanks for helping me out! [NPC] Shepherd: Please accept this as a token of my gratitude! +4 Pelts|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|After Shearing all [[Sheep]]|[NPC] Shepherd: My sheep are some of the best in the world!|
## Tips
- Shearing all the sheep at once is a difficult task, without a [[God Potion]] or other serious speed boosts.
- Players can gather all sheep using leads and bring them to just in front of the Shepherd's house to quickly shear all the sheep at the same time.
- Killing the sheep isn't helpful, as the player would have to wait for them to respawn.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 21, 2021|0.11.4|Added Shepherd.|
Linked from: [[Locations]], [[Locations/Mushroom Desert]], [[Mushroom Desert]], [[NPC/List/Mushroom Desert]], [[Pelts]], [[Shepherd's Keep]], [[Vanilla Objects]]

# Skulls
Categories: Non-player characters, Dungeons
[Skulls]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Serves as decorative blocks.
Location: The Catacombs ([[Catacombs]])
Skulls are decorative blocks in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] that can sometimes be clicked on to obtain [[NPC]] dialogue or a [[Redstone Key]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Wither Skull; (Random)|[SKULL] Wither Skull: That's a door, and I keep the door...there.|
|Wither Skull; (Random)|[SKULL] Wither Skull: You need a Wither Key to open this door!|
|Wither Skull; (Random)|[SKULL] Wither Skull: Death awaits you on this path, and everywhere else to be fair.|
|Wither Skull; (Random)|[SKULL] Wither Skull: Monsters with a star next to their name have Wither Keys...sometimes.|
|Wither Skull; (Random)|[SKULL] Wither Skull: This is the way.|
|Wither Skull; (Random)|[SKULL] Wither Skull: If you didn't find the Wither Key yet, it probably means you need to go back!|
|Green Skull; (Random)|[SKULL] Green Skull: I'm feeling a bit queasy.|
|Green Skull; (Random)|[SKULL] Green Skull: Gotta go green!|
|Green Skull; (Random)|[SKULL] Green Skull: Anyone else feel sick in here?|
|Green Skull; (Random)|[SKULL] Green Skull: Green would look good on you.|
|Blue Skull; (Random)|[SKULL] Blue Skull: Some guy called Pedro did this to us.|
|Blue Skull; (Random)|[SKULL] Blue Skull: I think we are all victims of a terrible joke.|
|Blue Skull; (Random)|[SKULL] Blue Skull: Wait I'm blue too?? I must have been sleeping.|
|Yellow Skull; (Random)|[SKULL] Yellow Skull: Touch my treasure and you will get it!|
|Yellow Skull; (Random)|[SKULL] Yellow Skull: Ah man, this is the death! I'm loving it.|
|Red Skull; (Random)|[SKULL] Red Skull: They all copied me.|
|Red Skull; (Random)|[SKULL] Red Skull: I'm not red, I'm magenta.|

# Skylark
Categories: Non-player characters
[Skylark]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Contemplates the nature of their existence.
Location: [[West Village]]
Coordinates: -71.5 71.15 -115
Skylark is an [[NPC]] located in the [[West Village]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[NPC] Yoshua: Do you ever feel like we're just programmed to do certain things in life? [NPC] Skylark: I used to, because it seems like everything you CAN do is pre-determined, like a computer program. [NPC] Skylark: Like an all-powerful entity created this world and everything that can exist in it. [NPC] Yoshua: What made you change your mind? [NPC] Skylark: Well, think of it like a video game. [NPC] Skylark: The main storyline may be fixed. [NPC] Skylark: But that doesn't stop the player from creating their own stories and experiences within the game. [NPC] Skylark: Especially when playing with others!|
## Trivia
- Skylark is one of 5 [[NPC]]'s to mention the "simulation" or the simulated state of the world of skyblock. Others include the [[Royal Resident]], the [[Cult of the Fallen Star]] members in meetings #4 - #6, [[Oen]], and [[Yoshua]].

Description: Rewards the player with a [[Treasure Chest]] after freeing them.
Location: [[Jungle]]
Sludge is an [[NPC]] found in the [[Jungle]] of the [[Crystal Hollows]] inside a small wooden structure. After hitting them 5 times, they reward the player with a chest of loot, containing [[Crystal Hollows|normal loot]] and high levels of [[Gemstone Powder]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Sludge: Hey you! Help me I'm stuck! [NPC] Sludge: I fell asleep and when I woke up I was too large to fit the entrance! [NPC] Sludge: Maybe you can punch me out.|
|After 5 Punches|[NPC] Sludge: Wow I wasn't expecting that! [NPC] Sludge: Thank you so much, I stayed there for weeks! [NPC] Sludge: You freed me, and helped me find the love of my life! [NPC] Sludge: Take this offering in return!|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|After Freeing|[NPC] Sludge: Thank you so much!|
|After Freeing|[NPC] Sludge: Maybe I feel such an intense connection with my other half because, it was literally my other half?|
## Trivia
- After they have been split, both smaller Sludges have 3 [[Health]] , have hearts surrounding them, and cannot be damaged.

# Solomon
Categories: Non-player characters, Lore
[Solomon]
Type: [[NPC]]
This page is about parts of the game's lore, its content may only appear in [[NPC]] dialogue, [[Journal|Dungeon Journals]], item descriptions or similar sources.
Solomon was a mage sent by a human king with the expedition to [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]]. The other two mages sent were the Royal Inscriptionist, who made the journal about the expedition, and [[Sadan]].
## Story
- In the year [[Time Systems|1531 AE]], Zombies appeared near the sealed Catacombs, and due to this, a human king decided to launch an expedition there in fear of [[The Wither King]]. He put [[General Dan]] in charge of the expedition, and sent a mages from his personal guard, including Solomon and [[Sadan]], with him. The group of 50 soldiers and 3 mages, one of which kept a journal of their progress, were to leave the next morning. Halfway through second floor of the Catacombs, one of their scouts noticed an irregularity — a tomb that had seemingly been looted, without the trace of any remains. [[General Dan]] ordered the men to rest in the crypt.
- The soldiers were, however, attacked by a Zombie, who killed one soldier and wounded another. It was then killed by Solomon. The expedition continued an hour after this attack, until they found another undead, a Wither Skeleton, who single-handedly killed 3 soldiers. Solomon suggested ending the expedition and reporting back to the king, with his theory that [[The Wither King]] had survived and does, in fact, control the Catacombs now. [[General Dan]] decided to explore the third floor, and then go back.
- At the end of the third floor, the humans encountered a large room with water pipes, and they took a break there and prepared their lunch. One of the soldiers yelled that enemies are coming, and they were quickly surrounded by hundreds of Wither Skeletons, Giant Spiders, Zombies and Skeletons. [[General Dan|Dan]] rallied the soldiers to a desperate fight, assisted by Solomon.
- In another journal kept by one of the soldiers, the author describes Solomon's and [[General Dan|Dan's]] bravery and how one of the mages, the one keeping the report, took an opportunity to escape. The mage stunned a group of enemies and made a run for it, followed by several soldiers. However, the escaping mage did not survive, and the last volume of their journal was purposely damaged. Meanwhile, another Wither Skeleton entered the room, attacking [[General Dan|Dan]] directly. Using his two-handed sword, he cut the Skeleton in half and its sword with it.
- Soon after, however, Solomon sensed something and warned everyone that they need to leave. A [[Necron|Wither]] entered the room and killed the [[General Dan|General]] with a single spell cast.
- After [[General Dan|Dan's]] death, Solomon urged the others to run, while casting a magic barrier, in an attempt to protect other soldiers from enemy's demonic spells.
(At some point he died, but that is not described in the journals.)
- In year 1532 AE, a year after the expedition, [[Storm]] absorbed Solomon to attain his third and final Wither Head, giving him immense power at the cost of his humanity.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jul 7, 2020|0.8|Added Journal Entries, which mention Solomon and the expedition.|
Linked from: [[General Dan]], [[Mentioned Entities]], [[Sadan]], [[Storm]], [[Time Systems]]

# Soma
Categories: Non-player characters
[Soma]
Type: [[NPC]]
[Locations]
Description: Complains that [[Barry]] will tax them too much.
Location: [[Barry Center]]
Coordinates: -17 104.062 -108
Description: Works for [[Barry]] after learning exactly how much they'll be taxed, and how it's less than they thought.
Location: [[Barry HQ]]
Coordinates: -52 54 -141
Soma is an [[NPC]] who first appears in the [[Barry Center]] in the [[Rift Dimension]]. They start out as a protestor against [[Barry]], but after the player handles their complaints, they move to [[Barry HQ]] and become a worker.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Before first interaction with [[Barry]]|[NPC] Soma: Barry is a terrible leader! [NPC] Soma: He doesn't want to hear us out!|
|Before first interaction with [[Barry]]|[NPC] Soma: Barry is hiding from us! [NPC] Soma: He doesn't want to face reality!|
|Before first interaction with [[Barry]]|[NPC] Soma: Someone promised donuts when coming to this protest. [NPC] Soma: And I don't see ANY DONUT! [NPC] Soma: Screw Barry!|
|After first interaction with [[Barry]]|[NPC] Soma: I earn a lot of Motes. I'm very rich. [NPC] Soma: I heard that Barry wanted to tax people who earn that much by 50%. [NPC] Soma: I need him to explain himself. I should tell Barry about Soma's complaints...|
|After telling Barry about Soma's complaints|[NPC] Soma: Fifty percent taxes!! [NPC] Soma: I earn 110,000 Motes per revolution. [NPC] Soma: What is going to happen? Select the appropriate Barry rhetoric: ① [You will have to pay 50% of that, so 55,000.] ② [You are in the highest tax bracket, but the tax system is progressive.] ③ [I don't know.]|
|Selecting ③ I don't know|[NPC] Soma: Well then go find out!!|
|Selecting ② [You are in the highest tax bracket, but the tax system is progressive.]|[NPC] Soma: I see. And how many brackets are there? Select an option: [3] [4] [5]|
|Selecting [5]|[NPC] Soma: Interesting, and can you tell me what are the Tax Brackets? Select an option: [They go from 0 to 20k, then 40k, 80k, and finally 100k+.] [They go from 0 to 20k, then 40k, 70k, and finally 100k+.] [They go from 0 to 20k, then 30k, 80k, and finally 90k+.]|
|Selecting [They go from 0 to 20k, then 40k, 70k, and finally 100k+.]|[NPC] Soma: Interesting. And what are the tax rates in each bracket? Select an option: [From first to last they are 15%, 25%, 35%, 45% and 50%.] [From first to last they are 30%, 35%, 40%, 45% and 50%.] [From first to last they are 10%, 20%, 30%, 40% and 50%.]|
|Selecting [From first to last they are 10%, 20%, 30%, 40% and 50%.]|[NPC] Soma: So how much will I be taxed? Considering I earn 110,000? Select an option: [5,000] [26,000] [28,000] [30,000] [32,000] [34,000] [36,000] [38,000] [40,000] [50,000] [55,000]|
|Selecting [32,000]|[NPC] Soma: Great! [NPC] Soma: It's not that bad after all! [NPC] Soma: I can totally afford if it's for the community.|
|Selecting an incorrect answer|[NPC] Soma: Hum, that can't be right...|
|In [[Barry HQ]]|[NPC] Soma: What are they spending the tax money on? [NPC] Soma: That is a mystery.|
## Trivia
- While in [[Barry Center]], Soma and the other protestors have dialogue above their heads, such as "Barry bad!", "Go away Barry!", "Barry is trash.", "Go away Barry!", "Barry boooo!", and "Barry sucks!"

## Quest
Sorcerer Okron asks the player to collect 8 Glyphs and imbue them onto a house, to defend it from a Dragon attack. After completing this quest, the player will receive the [[Warding Trinket]].

## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Before first interaction with [[Barry]]|[NPC] Stain: Barry is a terrible leader! [NPC] Stain: He doesn't want to hear us out!|

|Selecting ① [Berry said it's not true, he did something in his life. He just can't remember what.]|[NPC] Stain: I do not believe that. [NPC] Stain: I'm fairly certain he did zero. [NPC] Stain: Zilch. [NPC] Stain: Zip. [NPC] Stain: Zippo.|
|Selecting ② [Berry said that at least he's not called Stain.]|[NPC] Stain: Not cool. That's very low, even for Berry.|
|Selecting ③ [Berry said that his name is Barry.]|[NPC] Stain: Oh. [NPC] Stain: Ohhhhh! [NPC] Stain: Barry, the famous wizard! [NPC] Stain: Yeah! I love that guy!|
|In [[Barry HQ]]|[NPC] Stain: I'll help Berry until I die. [NPC] Stain: I mean Barry.|
## Trivia
- While in [[Barry Center]], Stain and the other protestors have dialogue above their heads, such as "Barry bad!", "Go away Barry!", "Barry is trash.", "Go away Barry!", "Barry boooo!", and "Barry sucks!"

|First Interaction|[NPC] Strux: How did you manage to get in here??? [NPC] Strux: Keep the trapdoors closed! [NPC] Strux: Magma Cube pets are forbidden in Dragontail! [NPC] Strux: Please don't tell anyone!!!|

# Susan
Categories: Non-player characters
[Susan]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Safety First ([[Quests]])
Description: Allows the player to access the [[Security Hall]].
Location: [[Community Center]]
Coordinates: -1.5 79 25.5
Susan is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Community Center]]. To access her associated features, players must be at least SkyBlock Level 10.
## Usage
Completion of Susan's dialogue grants access to the [[Security Hall]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Before reaching SkyBlock Level 10 ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|[NPC] Susan: You look like you're still getting the hang of things. Come back when you're SkyBlock Level 10!|
|First interaction|[NPC] Susan: Hey, are you here to volunteer for the Safety Squad? Select an option: [Absolutely!] [Tell me more about the Safety Squad.] [Not right now.]|
|Selecting Absolutely!|[NPC] Susan: Good heavens, that's great news! [NPC] Susan: With dangerous activity running rampant recently, we could really use your help. [NPC] Susan: You'll need to undergo some training to become a Safety Advocate. [NPC] Susan: Is this fine with you? I promise it won't take long. Select an option: [Sounds good with me!] [Give me a moment.]|
|Selecting [Tell me more about the Safety Squad.]|[NPC] Susan: The Safety Squad is dedicated to keeping SkyBlock safe. [NPC] Susan: A lot of incidents can be avoided if there just was more awareness about safety. [NPC] Susan: As such, we are recruiting Safety Advocates to help spread the importance of safety in SkyBlock. [NPC] Susan: Are you willing to become a Safety Advocate? [NPC] Susan: The world needs your help! Select an option: [Absolutely!] [Let me think about it a bit more.]|
|Selecting [Not right now.] or [Let me think about it a bit more.]|[NPC] Susan: That's fine, you can always come back later.|
|Selecting [Give me a moment.]|[NPC] Susan: Talk to me when you're ready.|
|Interacting after selecting [Give me a moment.]|[NPC] Susan: Are you ready for your Safety Advocate training? Select an option: [I'm good to go!] [Give me a moment.]|
|Selecting [Sounds good to me!] or [I'm good to go!]|[NPC] Susan: Processing your registration... Susan will repeatedly interact with the player while holding an Abiphone Basic ([[Abiphones]]). [NPC] Susan: Awesome! You're our #th volunteer! [NPC] Susan: Head into the doorway next to me, she'll be waiting for you!|
|Interacting again before following into the [[Security Hall]]|[NPC] Susan: Brigette is waiting for you in the Security Hall. Better not keep her waiting!|
|Future interactions|[NPC] Susan: Stay safe out there, recruit!|

[NPC] Swoop: Friends of my friends are my enemies - whoops, my friends I mean! [NPC] Swoop: You might be wondering why I'm on top of this bush? [NPC] Swoop: I'm camping it to get the berries before someone else. [NPC] Swoop: ... [NPC] Swoop: I'm joking! I would never do such thing, haha! [NPC] Swoop: Never. Select an option: [What's a berry?]|
|After selecting [What's a berry?]|[NPC] Swoop: See for yourself! Look, a berry just appeared, simply punch the bush to collect it!|
|Before collecting a [[Lushlilac]]|[NPC] Swoop: A berry just appeared, simply punch the bush to collect it!|
|After collecting a [[Lushlilac]]|[NPC] Swoop: Great! [NPC] Swoop: Berries are pretty useful to craft Hunting items. [NPC] Swoop: If you want to learn more about Hunting, you should talk to David Hunterborough next door. [NPC] Swoop: I'm more specialized in tree cutting. [NPC] Swoop: Have you ever heard about ∮ Sweep? Select an option: [Yes] [No]|
|Selecting [Yes]|[NPC] Swoop: Great! [NPC] Swoop: So you probably know that Sweep allows you to cut multiple logs at once. [NPC] Swoop: For example, 10∮ Sweep should allow you to cut 10 logs. [NPC] Swoop: Except that some Trees are tougher than others! [NPC] Swoop: If a Tree has a high Toughness, you will need more than 10∮ Sweep to cut 10 logs! [NPC] Swoop: This Fig Tree right next to us, has a Toughness of 7. [NPC] Swoop: So I suggest you use an axe with at least +7∮ Sweep. [NPC] Swoop: Here, let me give you this legendary axe that has been passed down my whole family tree for 12 generations.|
|Selecting [No]|[NPC] Swoop: I see, a complete newbie. [NPC] Swoop: Well, ∮ Sweep allows you to cut multiple logs at once. [NPC] Swoop: For example, 10∮ Sweep should allow you to cut 10 logs, you understand? Select an option: [So far so good]|
|Selecting [So far so good]|[NPC] Swoop: Except that some Trees are tougher than others! [NPC] Swoop: If a Tree has a high Toughness, you will need more than 10∮ Sweep to cut 10 logs! [NPC] Swoop: This Fig Tree right next to us, has a Toughness of 7. [NPC] Swoop: So I suggest you use an axe with at least +7∮ Sweep. [NPC] Swoop: Here, let me give you this legendary axe that has been passed down my whole family tree for 12 generations.|
|Claiming the [[Seriously Damaged Axe]]|[NPC] Swoop: Just kidding, this axe is terrible, but it will do for now. [NPC] Swoop: But there is more... [NPC] Swoop: The trees on this island are even tougher if they are not cut in the proper order. [NPC] Swoop: For example, you should cut the trunks of the Fig Trees first! [NPC] Swoop: Otherwise, you won't be that efficient at cutting them down. [NPC] Swoop: You can also throw some axes into the air by right-clicking with them in your hand. [NPC] Swoop: Have a go at cutting down this Fig Tree behind me.|
|Before cutting down a Fig Tree|[NPC] Swoop: You should cut the trunks of the Fig Trees first! [NPC] Swoop: Otherwise you will be much less efficient. [NPC] Swoop: Don't forget you can RIGHT-CLICK Foraging axes to throw them into the air! [NPC] Swoop: Here, go and cut a Fig Tree.|
|After cutting a Fig Tree|[NPC] Swoop: Not bad! [NPC] Swoop: You may have noticed already, but cutting down a tree here will grant you a Tree Gift. [NPC] Swoop: Tree Gifts are rewarded to everyone who helped to cut down a tree. [NPC] Swoop: Cutting down enough Fig or Mangrove Trees will level up your milestones and grant extra ∮ Sweep. [NPC] Swoop: This means that it's better to group together to cut down trees around here. [NPC] Swoop: There is a lot more to know about cutting down trees, so talk to me if you want to learn more.|

# Tal Ker
Aliases: Talker
Categories: Non-player characters
[Tal Ker]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Talks for a very long time.
Location: [[Grand Library]]
Coordinates: 193.5 196 205.5
Tal Ker is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Grand Library]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]]. They have 173 lines of dialogue and give no reward once finished with it. The player must click for each line, or alternatively hold right click. After the dialogue is finished, Tal Ker will not talk again upon clicking him.
## Dialogue
Note: After requesting time to think, the second dialogue will appear after the Daily [[NPC]] Shop Reset.
### First Time
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Interaction 1|[NPC] Tal Ker: Hey!|
|Interaction 2|[NPC] Tal Ker: I'm the guy that never stops talking|
|Interaction 3|[NPC] Tal Ker: All I do is say stuff|
|Interaction 4|[NPC] Tal Ker: I'm not entirely sure why but I can't help it|
|Interaction 5|[NPC] Tal Ker: There really is no point talking to me|
|Interaction 6|[NPC] Tal Ker: I won't give you anything|
|Interaction 7|[NPC] Tal Ker: I won't even say anything interesting|
|Interaction 8|[NPC] Tal Ker: I just talk about random things|
|Interaction 9|[NPC] Tal Ker: Like for example|
|Interaction 10|[NPC] Tal Ker: The other day I met some kind of wizard|
|Interaction 11|[NPC] Tal Ker: And they told me to come back in 3 to 5 business days|
|Interaction 12|[NPC] Tal Ker: That was over a year ago|
|Interaction 13|[NPC] Tal Ker: Still nothing|
|Interaction 14|[NPC] Tal Ker: What kind of people do that?|
|Interaction 15|[NPC] Tal Ker: Tells you to come back in a few days for no reason|
|Interaction 16|[NPC] Tal Ker: Really makes you think|
|Interaction 17|[NPC] Tal Ker: Also this guy, Crappox or something|
|Interaction 18|[NPC] Tal Ker: Told me to kill enemy monsters for him|
|Interaction 19|[NPC] Tal Ker: I asked him how much he'd pay me|
|Interaction 20|[NPC] Tal Ker: He said I had to pay him instead|
|Interaction 21|[NPC] Tal Ker: Makes no sense to me|
|Interaction 22|[NPC] Tal Ker: I met a guy called Jerry too|
|Interaction 23|[NPC] Tal Ker: Smart guy, he's going places|
|Interaction 24|[NPC] Tal Ker: Met him a few times in fact, he's always around|
|Interaction 25|[NPC] Tal Ker: Not the best for conversations but|
|Interaction 26|[NPC] Tal Ker: At least he's a good listener|
|Interaction 27|[NPC] Tal Ker: And I like that, good listeners|
|Interaction 28|[NPC] Tal Ker: But you know what I don't like listening to?|
|Interaction 29|[NPC] Tal Ker: People who play that harp|
|Interaction 30|[NPC] Tal Ker: You know the one I'm talking about?|
|Interaction 31|[NPC] Tal Ker: Always people playing it, but terrible music|
|Interaction 32|[NPC] Tal Ker: Sometimes they even stop in the middle of the song|
|Interaction 33|[NPC] Tal Ker: And they restart, just like that|
|Interaction 34|[NPC] Tal Ker: Go figures|
|Interaction 35|[NPC] Tal Ker: There's also this guy who's asking me to race him sometimes|
|Interaction 36|[NPC] Tal Ker: He's acting like he's the best|
|Interaction 37|[NPC] Tal Ker: But I always beat him first try|
|Interaction 38|[NPC] Tal Ker: What's his problem?|
|Interaction 39|[NPC] Tal Ker: At least he's an original|
|Interaction 40|[NPC] Tal Ker: Most people always talk about coins around here|
|Interaction 41|[NPC] Tal Ker: Everything has a value, and time is money|
|Interaction 42|[NPC] Tal Ker: People don't take their time to enjoy the little things|
|Interaction 43|[NPC] Tal Ker: Even you I'm sure|
|Interaction 44|[NPC] Tal Ker: You really think I'm going to reward you with something|
|Interaction 45|[NPC] Tal Ker: But I'm not, I swear|
|Interaction 46|[NPC] Tal Ker: You can leave at any time and it won't affect anything|
|Interaction 47|[NPC] Tal Ker: I just like to talk, I can go on for hours|
|Interaction 48|[NPC] Tal Ker: Someone told me I was interesting once|
|Interaction 49|[NPC] Tal Ker: Didn't believe them one bit|
|Interaction 50|[NPC] Tal Ker: So many people lying and trying to be your friend|
|Interaction 51|[NPC] Tal Ker: They all have a motive|
|Interaction 52|[NPC] Tal Ker: Especially those mayor candidates|
|Interaction 53|[NPC] Tal Ker: All they want is your vote|
|Interaction 54|[NPC] Tal Ker: But do they really care about me?|
|Interaction 55|[NPC] Tal Ker: Doubt [x]|
|Interaction 56|[NPC] Tal Ker: It's always so black and white with them|
|Interaction 57|[NPC] Tal Ker: Mining, fishing, they never mix|
|Interaction 58|[NPC] Tal Ker: Why is that? What if I like mining and fishing?|
|Interaction 59|[NPC] Tal Ker: But there is no point arguing|
|Interaction 60|[NPC] Tal Ker: They are always right|
|Interaction 61|[NPC] Tal Ker: And the people, sheep I say|
|Interaction 62|[NPC] Tal Ker: They always vote for the same|
|Interaction 63|[NPC] Tal Ker: It's always a landslide|
|Interaction 64|[NPC] Tal Ker: Everyone wants to do like everyone else|
|Interaction 65|[NPC] Tal Ker: Not me, I'm a free spirit|
|Interaction 66|[NPC] Tal Ker: I'll tell you why|
|Interaction 67|[NPC] Tal Ker: My home is clean and beautiful|
|Interaction 68|[NPC] Tal Ker: Is your island beautiful?|
|Interaction 69|[NPC] Tal Ker: A proper island is the key to success|
|Interaction 70|[NPC] Tal Ker: Healthy island leads to healthy life|
|Interaction 71|[NPC] Tal Ker: Also, remember those island we could place?|
|Interaction 72|[NPC] Tal Ker: Like the sand one and forest one?|
|Interaction 73|[NPC] Tal Ker: What happened to those, why not more?|
|Interaction 74|[NPC] Tal Ker: So weird, it looked promising|
|Interaction 75|[NPC] Tal Ker: Instead you have me|
|Interaction 76|[NPC] Tal Ker: Sometimes I wonder|
|Interaction 77|[NPC] Tal Ker: If I wasn't talking, what would people do instead|
|Interaction 78|[NPC] Tal Ker: Am I a waste of time and space|
|Interaction 79|[NPC] Tal Ker: Is that why I'm here, on this world|
|Interaction 80|[NPC] Tal Ker: Maybe it's time I do something with my life|
|Interaction 81|[NPC] Tal Ker: I could run for mayor|
|Interaction 82|[NPC] Tal Ker: I wonder if people would vote for me|
|Interaction 83|[NPC] Tal Ker: What would my program even be?|
|Interaction 84|[NPC] Tal Ker: I would reward people for talking|
|Interaction 85|[NPC] Tal Ker: But not those who talk about their auctions, yuck|
|Interaction 86|[NPC] Tal Ker: I would reward people who explore and walk around|
|Interaction 87|[NPC] Tal Ker: I would fill the village with friends with amazing stories to tell|
|Interaction 88|[NPC] Tal Ker: But who would vote for me|
|Interaction 89|[NPC] Tal Ker: Maybe instead I should stay there and keep talking|
|Interaction 90|[NPC] Tal Ker: Talking is my favorite event|
|Interaction 91|[NPC] Tal Ker: I like events but I prefer uneventful days|
|Interaction 92|[NPC] Tal Ker: That way I can keep talking|
|Interaction 93|[NPC] Tal Ker: I can't believe you are still listening|
|Interaction 94|[NPC] Tal Ker: You are one of a kind|
|Interaction 95|[NPC] Tal Ker: I have many more stories up my sleeve|
|Interaction 96|[NPC] Tal Ker: Like that time I found a fairy soul|
|Interaction 97|[NPC] Tal Ker: I went to Tia after because she asked me to|
|Interaction 98|[NPC] Tal Ker: So I asked her, what are fairy souls?|
|Interaction 99|[NPC] Tal Ker: And she said they are the remains of dead fairies|
|Interaction 100|[NPC] Tal Ker: Isn't that terrible?! So many dead fairies everywhere!|
|Interaction 101|[NPC] Tal Ker: She also said that all those fairies didn't die a peaceful death.|
|Interaction 102|[NPC] Tal Ker: They were all killed! A long time ago.|
|Interaction 103|[NPC] Tal Ker: But why would anyone do that? And who did it?|
|Interaction 104|[NPC] Tal Ker: The strangest part is, Tia really didn't seem phased about it.|
|Interaction 105|[NPC] Tal Ker: Just thinking about it I have a chill down my spine.|
|Interaction 106|[NPC] Tal Ker: Those fairies are so strange.|
|Interaction 107|[NPC] Tal Ker: But you know what else is strange?|
|Interaction 108|[NPC] Tal Ker: Accessories, I mean what are those|
|Interaction 109|[NPC] Tal Ker: I have an entire bag full of accessories|
|Interaction 110|[NPC] Tal Ker: I'm basically a garage sale at this point|
|Interaction 111|[NPC] Tal Ker: My favorite one is the cat one|
|Interaction 112|[NPC] Tal Ker: I love cats|
|Interaction 113|[NPC] Tal Ker: I want a cat pet but not the black one, bad memories|
|Interaction 114|[NPC] Tal Ker: Cats are the best, they listen to me|
|Interaction 115|[NPC] Tal Ker: I think they are learning, one day they will take control|
|Interaction 116|[NPC] Tal Ker: But be my guest, a cat mayor is all I need|
|Interaction 117|[NPC] Tal Ker: My second favorite activity is sleeping|
|Interaction 118|[NPC] Tal Ker: But even in my sleep, I dream of talking|
|Interaction 119|[NPC] Tal Ker: Sometimes I dream that I'm talking to myself|
|Interaction 120|[NPC] Tal Ker: A whole table with 10 clones of me around it|
|Interaction 121|[NPC] Tal Ker: All of us pretending to be more interesting than the others|
|Interaction 122|[NPC] Tal Ker: But how could we? We are all the same|
|Interaction 123|[NPC] Tal Ker: I guess my dreams are not very creative|
|Interaction 124|[NPC] Tal Ker: I don't have a great imagination, that's why I never lie|
|Interaction 125|[NPC] Tal Ker: I wonder though, do you think there will be another world like this one day?|
|Interaction 126|[NPC] Tal Ker: Somewhere else, not exactly the same, but better in some way|
|Interaction 127|[NPC] Tal Ker: Also it sounds crazy but, do you think we could exist without cubes?|
|Interaction 128|[NPC] Tal Ker: Ender Pearls are my favorite items|
|Interaction 129|[NPC] Tal Ker: Not because of what they do, but because they are circles|
|Interaction 130|[NPC] Tal Ker: I wish the world was a circle too, it's so much prettier|
|Interaction 131|[NPC] Tal Ker: I'd love to be in a circle world|
|Interaction 132|[NPC] Tal Ker: Maybe there is a circle world somewhere, with someone just like me|
|Interaction 133|[NPC] Tal Ker: In their circle world, dreaming of a cubic world|
|Interaction 134|[NPC] Tal Ker: You really are a great listener|
|Interaction 135|[NPC] Tal Ker: Now I regret saying I wouldn't reward you|
|Interaction 136|[NPC] Tal Ker: But I swore, and I am a man of my words|
|Interaction 137|[NPC] Tal Ker: But thank you for listening, I appreciate it a lot|
|Interaction 138|[NPC] Tal Ker: I hope that you are happy and that everything is good in your life|
|Interaction 139|[NPC] Tal Ker: There are ups and down sometimes but there is always a shinning light|
|Interaction 140|[NPC] Tal Ker: My shinning light is you, a beam of hope in this dark cave|
|Interaction 141|[NPC] Tal Ker: I'll tell you the story of my life|
|Interaction 142|[NPC] Tal Ker: Once upon a time, on an island far away|
|Interaction 143|[NPC] Tal Ker: I used to live in a small town, we had a house, 2 floors and an attic|
|Interaction 144|[NPC] Tal Ker: Back then I was quite shy and reserved|
|Interaction 145|[NPC] Tal Ker: I liked reading and listening to birds|
|Interaction 146|[NPC] Tal Ker: There was always a cat around the house, sometimes killing the birds|
|Interaction 147|[NPC] Tal Ker: I hated that cat with a passion, but my parents seemed to enjoy its presence|
|Interaction 148|[NPC] Tal Ker: One day the cat gave birth to 3 little devils, and decided to hide them in our attic|
|Interaction 149|[NPC] Tal Ker: They were making so much noise, right above my room|
|Interaction 150|[NPC] Tal Ker: I never understood the reason we had to leave them be, I wanted them out|
|Interaction 151|[NPC] Tal Ker: One day after sundown, a fire started spreading in our house|
|Interaction 152|[NPC] Tal Ker: Quickly we understood it was too late to stop it, and exited the house with our valuables|
|Interaction 153|[NPC] Tal Ker: I was asked if I knew whether or not the kittens were still inside the house|
|Interaction 154|[NPC] Tal Ker: When asked that question, I remained silent, but I knew the answer|
|Interaction 155|[NPC] Tal Ker: Soon after, the flames were engulfing the house|
|Interaction 156|[NPC] Tal Ker: Suddenly the mom cat appeared from the shadows of the night and ran into the house|
|Interaction 157|[NPC] Tal Ker: This creature usually so afraid of the fire, rushed into the infernal flames|
|Interaction 158|[NPC] Tal Ker: About 20 seconds later, the cat passed the front door again, holding one of their cubs|
|Interaction 159|[NPC] Tal Ker: And immediately after, dashed back inside the fire to save the rest of their litter|
|Interaction 160|[NPC] Tal Ker: After the second trip, the cat was burning, but still went back in, right after making sure the cub was safe|
|Interaction 161|[NPC] Tal Ker: I waited 30 seconds, my eyes fixed on the front door, holding my breath|
|Interaction 162|[NPC] Tal Ker: Finally it came out with the last kitten|
|Interaction 163|[NPC] Tal Ker: The cat was in terrible shape, but the kittens were safe|
|Interaction 164|[NPC] Tal Ker: It looked one last time at them, and died next to the 3 little bodies|
|Interaction 165|[NPC] Tal Ker: I was in shambles, never in my life had I been so wrong|
|Interaction 166|[NPC] Tal Ker: Later on I buried the cat under my favorite tree, and adopted their cubs|
|Interaction 167|[NPC] Tal Ker: I loved those 3 little devils with all my heart and gave them all the care in the world|
|Interaction 168|[NPC] Tal Ker: They became beautiful and happy cats, their mom would have been proud|
|Interaction 169|[NPC] Tal Ker: This event changed me to my very core, everything about me had to change|
|Interaction 170|[NPC] Tal Ker: From that day I never hated anything anymore, and never stopped talking|
|Interaction 171|[NPC] Tal Ker: But having said this story, I'll exceptionally need to take a break|
|Interaction 172|[NPC] Tal Ker: I haven't told that story to many people|
|Interaction 173|[NPC] Tal Ker: Thank you for listening to me|

### Second Time
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Interaction 1 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Oh you came back!|
|Interaction 2 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: For a moment I thought you wouldn't|
|Interaction 3 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I'm sorry if I spooked you a little bit last time|
|Interaction 4 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Sometimes I get carried away too much and tell personal stories|
|Interaction 5 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I apologize|
|Interaction 6 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: You can call me Tal, some people call me TK|
|Interaction 7 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I don't actually live there, I'm just visiting|
|Interaction 8 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Maybe one day I’ll show you my place|
|Interaction 9 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I think I had dwarves somewhere in my family tree|
|Interaction 10 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: That's why I look like one|
|Interaction 11 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I like visiting islands, some more than others|
|Interaction 12 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Golden Mines, Crimson Isle... haven't been there in ages|
|Interaction 13 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I used to go to the End a lot, not anymore|
|Interaction 14 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: The dragon heart corrupts everything, even the people|
|Interaction 15 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I like it better down here, there are people like you|
|Interaction 16 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: The story of these mines is old, older than the End island for sure!|
|Interaction 17 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: You do know the story of the End island right?|
|Interaction 18 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Hum, well that's awkward|
|Interaction 19 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I guess they don't really teach you much do they|
|Interaction 20 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Unfortunately I don't know enough details myself|
|Interaction 21 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I do know a few things, you remember the giant skeleton on the island nearby?|
|Interaction 22 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: The heart of that creature is sealed on the End island|
|Interaction 23 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Not many people are left, who can tell the story of that day|
|Interaction 24 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Maybe Jerry saw it, when he was very young|
|Interaction 25 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: And even he was probably born much later|
|Interaction 26 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: The Calamity is the reason why the earth got shattered into many islands|
|Interaction 27 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I wish I knew more, it's a fantastic subject|
|Interaction 28 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: The end of an era, and the beginning of a much darker one|
|Interaction 29 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Sorry if I'm boring you, I like history|
|Interaction 30 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I went to the Sky Academy, greater teachers there|
|Interaction 31 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: You should go there someday, even greater adventurers need to learn a few things!|
|Interaction 32 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: My parents always told me to focus on my studies|
|Interaction 33 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: They were right, I'm a successful young man now!|
|Interaction 34 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Maybe not so young, but my point still stands|
|Interaction 35 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Studies on one side and fun on the other, both are very important|
|Interaction 36 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Yin and yang, one should not go without the other|
|Interaction 37 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: The Academy was tough, but I met a few friends there|
|Interaction 38 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: You know the banker?|
|Interaction 39 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: He was in my promotion back then|
|Interaction 40 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: He invented the bank!|
|Interaction 41 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I still have no idea where all that interest is coming from|
|Interaction 42 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: A genius this guy|
|Interaction 43 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I like seeing the world evolve through time|
|Interaction 44 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: What they did to the village a few years ago was great|
|Interaction 45 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I feel like we entered the modern age|
|Interaction 46 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: The technology is progressing fast, who knows what's coming next!|
|Interaction 47 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I'm sure one day people will fly to the moon|
|Interaction 48 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I knew a time without adventurers, without banks and auctions|
|Interaction 49 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Not much was happening back then|
|Interaction 50 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: And then it happened|
|Interaction 51 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: One day hordes of people appeared out of nowhere|
|Interaction 52 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Nobody seems to know why|
|Interaction 53 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I have my theory but I won't tell you|
|Interaction 54 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: You would think I'm crazy, maybe you already do|
|Interaction 55 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: All those people at once|
|Interaction 56 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: It was really overwhelming|
|Interaction 57 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: It did create so many business opportunities|
|Interaction 58 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: The economy is booming|
|Interaction 59 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: That guy, Bazaar, he really took advantage of it|
|Interaction 60 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Bazaar isn't even his real name|
|Interaction 61 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: He changed his name to Bazaar because it was better for business, he said|
|Interaction 62 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: A real specimen|
|Interaction 63 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Lots of people think they understand the market|
|Interaction 64 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I gave up on it|
|Interaction 65 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I did buy some stocks of stonks when he opened|
|Interaction 66 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I still have a few|
|Interaction 67 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: That Warren guy made a lot of money and disappeared|
|Interaction 68 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Another shark|
|Interaction 69 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Maybe I should sell my own currency and make people believe it's worth many coins|
|Interaction 70 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Like Talcoins?|
|Interaction 71 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: The value of anything is in the eyes of the buyers|
|Interaction 72 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I'm sure I could raise the price of anything if I really tried|
|Interaction 73 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Some people do that all the time in the community|
|Interaction 74 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I like it though, it creates opportunities for everyone|
|Interaction 75 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I think a lot of what happens around here can serve as a life lesson|
|Interaction 76 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: It's important to experience the right things when you are young|
|Interaction 77 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Even bad moments can be blessings in disguise|
|Interaction 78 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Someone scammed me a very valuable thing one day|
|Interaction 79 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: They even got away with it, awful|
|Interaction 80 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: But never again have I gotten fooled|
|Interaction 81 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: If I could I would pay him double|
|Interaction 82 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: A lesson for a few coins, a great deal|
|Interaction 83 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Always stay vigilant|
|Interaction 84 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Some people don't see it the same way|
|Interaction 85 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Do you know the origin of the word utopia?|
|Interaction 86 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: It means a place that does not exist|
|Interaction 87 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Even the people who created the word knew it was a paradox|
|Interaction 88 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Some people are smarter than hundreds of other combined|
|Interaction 89 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Do you think they were born that way, or became?|
|Interaction 90 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I have my own view on the matter|
|Interaction 91 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: My opinion is that it's a little bit of both|
|Interaction 92 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: The smartest baby would become a monkey if it wasn't taught anything|
|Interaction 93 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Someone told me a story one day|
|Interaction 94 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Bird eggs are grey and dirty, snake eggs are white and clean|
|Interaction 95 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: The bird decided to sit on the clean egg instead of his|
|Interaction 96 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: The egg hatched and the snake ate the bird|
|Interaction 97 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: He told me the moral of the story is that birds are stupid|
|Interaction 98 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: But is that the moral of the story?|
|Interaction 99 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Maybe it's about appearances being deceiving|
|Interaction 100 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: A clean shell can hide a dirty mind|
|Interaction 101 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Maybe it's about beauty being subjective|
|Interaction 102 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: If the bird found dirty eggs pretty, he wouldn't have gotten in that situation|
|Interaction 103 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Maybe it's about planning ahead|
|Interaction 104 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Short term comfort can lead to troubles in the future|
|Interaction 105 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: Maybe it's about trusting your family more than others|
|Interaction 106 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: If you tell the same story to multiple people they might understand it differently|
|Interaction 107 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: What do you think is the moral of the story?|
|Interaction 108 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I love talking, but I also think a lot!|
|Interaction 109 After Returning|[NPC] Tal Ker: I'm going to think for a bit, thanks for listening to me! See you!|
### As a Garden Visitor
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|In [[The Garden]] First Interaction|[NPC] Tal Ker: I'm not really in the mood to talk right now [NPC] Tal Ker: At least not right now [NPC] Tal Ker: I'm all business today [NPC] Tal Ker: Things to do, places to be|
|In [[The Garden]] Accepting Offer|[NPC] Tal Ker: Thanks for the lovely flowers [NPC] Tal Ker: They're a gift to myself, sort of [NPC] Tal Ker: You see, recently I spoke to the wizard up in his big tower [NPC] Tal Ker: He told me that he finally got his portal to work [NPC] Tal Ker: And that he knew of an alternate version of himself who existed, stuck in another world [NPC] Tal Ker: I wonder if there is an alternate version of myself out there too [NPC] Tal Ker: But would he still really be me? [NPC] Tal Ker: If he has lived in such a different world and experienced so many different things [NPC] Tal Ker: Is he still me by anything other than name? [NPC] Tal Ker: I am Tal Ker, and I speak often and loud [NPC] Tal Ker: But what if he is Tal Ker, who speaks little and softly [NPC] Tal Ker: What if he has lived an entire life in silence [NPC] Tal Ker: Not feeling like anyone cares for what he has to say [NPC] Tal Ker: So he simply does not speak [NPC] Tal Ker: In that case, are we still the same Tal Ker? [NPC] Tal Ker: Maybe he speaks through song [NPC] Tal Ker: And passes time by playing the piano [NPC] Tal Ker: Maybe he's been playing for so long that he's gotten really good at it [NPC] Tal Ker: And, if we ever met, maybe we would just sit in silence [NPC] Tal Ker: And he would play me a song [NPC] Tal Ker: And he would share with me tales of his life, and his strange world, all without saying a word. [NPC] Tal Ker: Maybe if I went to this alternate dimension I would become like him [NPC] Tal Ker: Maybe I would speak less, think more [NPC] Tal Ker: And maybe you would come along one day [NPC] Tal Ker: And tell me that I've changed [NPC] Tal Ker: And maybe I would tell you that I have never been more me [NPC] Tal Ker: Because maybe I shouldn't worry that my alternate self isn't enough like me [NPC] Tal Ker: Maybe I should worry that I'm not more like my alternate self [NPC] Tal Ker: You know?|
|In [[The Garden]] Declining Offer|[NPC] Tal Ker: I wonder what your alternate self is like [NPC] Tal Ker: Maybe they are a florist [NPC] Tal Ker: And they give out flowers to everyone, even if they can't afford them [NPC] Tal Ker: Maybe that's why you are short on flowers in this life [NPC] Tal Ker: Because your other self gave them all away|
|In [[The Garden]] Using a Vacuum on him after accepting offer|[NPC] Tal Ker: I am not a pest [NPC] Tal Ker: But given that you see me as such, I shall depart. [NPC] Tal Ker: Adieu!|
## Trivia
- Tal Ker's name is a play on the word "talker".
- It takes an average of 6 minutes to talk to them with his full dialogue. This can be skipped by trying to [[Vacuums|Vacuum]] him while he talks.
- Crappox, guy mentioned by Tal Ker who told him to kill enemy monsters for him is likely [[Maddox the Slayer]].
  - He also refers to NPCs such as [[Wizard]], [[Jerry]], an unspecified [[Island Racing]] NPC, and [[Tia the Fairy]], as well as features such as [[Harp]], [[Mayor Election|Mayor Elections]], [[Locations|Islands]], [[Accessories]], [[Cat Talisman]] and [[Black Cat Pet]].
- Unlike most other NPCs, his dialogue progress is remembered if the player switches lobbies.

|During [[Detective Amog]]'s first reverse-quest|[NPC] Taylor: If you set aside her unconsolable grief, she really looks FAN-TAS-TIC!|
|During [[Detective Amog]]'s second reverse-quest|[NPC] Taylor: I truly hope Ramero survives. [NPC] Taylor: I doubt Joliet will buy the dress otherwise.|

# Tel Kar
Categories: Non-player characters
[Tel Kar]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Talks about the [[Rift Dimension]] and gives an Enigma Soul ([[Enigma Souls]]).
Location: [[Wyld Woods]]
Coordinates: -113.31 69 62.281
Tel Kar is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Wyld Woods]]. Talking with him fully grants an [[Enigma Souls|Enigma Soul]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Less than 2:30 [[Rift Time]]|[NPC] Tel Kar: Hello. [NPC] Tel Kar: I would love to talk with you. [NPC] Tel Kar: But I don't think you have the time. [NPC] Tel Kar: Let's talk next time we meet.|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Tel Kar: Hello. [NPC] Tel Kar: I'm Tel Kar. [NPC] Tel Kar: You must be wondering what exactly this place is. Select an option: [Do you know anything about it?]|
|Selecting [Do you know anything about it?]|[NPC] Tel Kar: I do! [NPC] Tel Kar: This is an alternate dimension. [NPC] Tel Kar: You arrived here through the portal, right? Select an option: [Yes, how do you know?]|
|Selecting [Yes, how do you know?]|[NPC] Tel Kar: That's because unlike me, you are not trapped in here. [NPC] Tel Kar: You are what I call a "tourist". Select an option: [Why are you trapped?]|
|Selecting [Why are you trapped?]|[NPC] Tel Kar: Because I made the mistake of entering through a breach! [NPC] Tel Kar: You see, there is a breach near the portal you entered, in your world. [NPC] Tel Kar: As a dwarf, I spend considerable time exploring caves and mountains. [NPC] Tel Kar: And I stepped into it by accident! [NPC] Tel Kar: Anyway... [NPC] Tel Kar: Any breach has an extremely large impact on causality. This is why everything is so strange. [NPC] Tel Kar: Until that breach is tamed, anything that steps through it becomes a paradox and remains TRAPPED. Select an option: [How do we tame it?]|
|Selecting [How do we tame it?]|[NPC] Tel Kar: I'm not certain, but I have a theory. [NPC] Tel Kar: I think you'll find the answer to that question at the top of the mountain, right here in the Rift. [NPC] Tel Kar: However, it won't be easy getting all the way up there. Select an option: [Why?]|
|Selecting [Why?]|[NPC] Tel Kar: Uh... [NPC] Tel Kar: How do I explain... [NPC] Tel Kar: You know about the three primary dimensions, right? [NPC] Tel Kar: Up-down. [NPC] Tel Kar: Left-right. [NPC] Tel Kar: Forward-backward. [NPC] Tel Kar: Right? [NPC] Tel Kar: They're also known as x, y and z. [NPC] Tel Kar: Well... [NPC] Tel Kar: Well, what if I told you this place has FIVE dimensions? Select an option: [Ok. What is the 4th one?]|
|Selecting [Ok. What is the 4th one?]|[NPC] Tel Kar: It's usually recognized that TIME is the 4th dimension. [NPC] Tel Kar: But time does not flow in the Rift. [NPC] Tel Kar: You can use time around here just as well as you can move in 3D in your world, as if you can interact with the space-time continuum itself. [NPC] Tel Kar: It's a relatively superhot concept in physics. Select an option: [I'm following. What is the 5th dimension?]|
|Selecting [I'm following. What is the 5th dimension?]|[NPC] Tel Kar: It is the only constant of SkyBlock... [NPC] Tel Kar: Randomness! [NPC] Tel Kar: Which is more accurately defined as possibilities. [NPC] Tel Kar: Most people and things have a reflection in other universes. [NPC] Tel Kar: Infinite reflections, one for each possibility. [NPC] Tel Kar: In your world, randomness is pre-defined. [NPC] Tel Kar: Only affected by powers like Magic Find. [NPC] Tel Kar: But here? [NPC] Tel Kar: Anything is possible. Select an option: [Sure, but what about the mountain?]|
|Selecting [Sure, but what about the mountain?]|[NPC] Tel Kar: I was just about to explain! [NPC] Tel Kar: The closer you get to the top of the mountain, the stronger the distortion gets. [NPC] Tel Kar: Basically, the mountain might seem close to you in three dimensions... [NPC] Tel Kar: But in reality, it is very very far away in five dimensions! Select an option: [How do I reach it then?] [NPC] Tel Kar: No one knows for sure. [NPC] Tel Kar: But as you may have noticed, as you experience the Rift, you gain control over more and more time. [NPC] Tel Kar: So if you interact with everything in here, it might lead you to the mountain! [NPC] Tel Kar: Do you have anymore questions? Select an option: [Yes. Do you have a soul for me?]|
|Selecting [Yes. Do you have a soul for me?]|[NPC] Tel Kar: What a coincidence! [NPC] Tel Kar: I do! [NPC] Tel Kar: See how this universe is filled with randomness? [NPC] Tel Kar: Take it! [NPC] Tel Kar: I loved talking with you!|
|After claiming Enigma Soul ([[Enigma Souls]])|[NPC] Tel Kar: I would love talking more with you, but I'm not sure I have the time. [NPC] Tel Kar: It's a joke... I have infinite time! [NPC] Tel Kar: In fact I'm the exact opposite of you... [NPC] Tel Kar: Time is the only thing I have! [NPC] Tel Kar: Anyway, see you around.|
## Trivia
- Tel Kar's dialogue effectively confirms, that those who enter [[Rift Dimension]] through [[The Breach]] rather than through [[Wizard Tower|Wizard Portal]] are unable to ever leave.
- Tel Kar is one of a few NPCs who are not originally from [[Rift Dimension]], alongside [[Dr. Emmett]], [[Barry]], [[Alatar]] and [[Julian]].

[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 4, 2023|Added Tel Kar.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Tel Kar.|

# Three Bears
Aliases: 3 Bears, Bear Story, Bear story, Ede, Keith, Pete
Categories: Non-player characters, Crystal Hollows
[Three Bears]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Tells the player a story, and then turns into a bear at the end.
Location: [[Crystal Hollows]]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Tells the player a story, and then turns into a bear at the end.
Location: [[Crystal Hollows]]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Tells the player a story, and then turns into a bear at the end.
Location: [[Crystal Hollows]]
The Three Bears are a group of [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the [[Crystal Hollows]]. They begin as Pete, Keith, and Ede and tell the player a story. At the end of the story, the player is given the blindness effect and the NPCs turn into bears and disappear.
Once they have been spoken to, Pete will have a chance to visit the player's [[The Garden|Garden]] in his bear form.
## Dialogue
- Grammar and/or punctuation mistakes are intentional, as they appear so in the dialogue in-game.
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Pete: Pal, you seem a lil' lost over there, [NPC] Keith: Why don'tcha come by the fire and sit down anywhere [NPC] Ede: I gotcha ya a good story piling up, but beware! [NPC] Pete: It's the scary story about the big bad bear [NPC] Keith: There was once a baby bear, little and cute, [NPC] Ede: but her parents, oh no!, they were quite the brute [NPC] Pete: they'd always throw tantrums about what and who, [NPC] Keith: but the lil' bear had enough and said "that's my cue!" [NPC] Ede: so the lil' fellow went out and about into the wood, [NPC] Pete: but what came of that, let me tell you, it didn't end very good [NPC] Keith: she found an empty cabin, rusty and old, [NPC] Ede: "a bit of rest wouldn't hurt" she said very bold [NPC] Pete: "so he sat down by the chimney with paws freezing cold, [NPC] Keith: and threw some wood and watched the fire unfold [NPC] Ede: he tugged himself in and went right to sleep, [NPC] Pete: but then something happened, the fire went out and he heard a loud creak [NPC] Keith: he got up and looked deep down the darkness [NPC] Ede: but what he then saw...|
|Pete in [[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Pete: Horror stories are better when you have something to snack on y'know? If you help us gather some ingredients, we could roast some tasty marshmallows.|
|Pete in [[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Pete: Thanks Pal! Why don't you sit around the campfire with us and enjoy these tasty treats?|
|Pete in [[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Pete: No supplies? You wouldn't want to experience what the lil' bear did, would you?|
## Gallery
- The skin that the NPCs turn into after the story is the same as the [[Spirit Bear]].
- The bear that the NPCs turn into after the story is told
## Trivia
- Keith's skin is the same as [[Fragilis]].

# Tia the Fairy
Aliases: Tia, Tia The Fairy
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Fairy Souls, Quest giver
[Tia the Fairy]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: [[Fairy Souls]]
Description: Introduces the player to [[Fairy Souls]], and allows them to exchange souls for rewards.
Location: [[Wilderness]]
Coordinates: 119.5 65 147.5
Tia the Fairy is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Wilderness]]. They provide the player with stat boosts in exchange for [[Fairy Souls]].
## Quest
For every five [[Fairy Souls]] that the [[Player]] exchanges with Tia, they will recieve [[SkyBlock Levels|10 SkyBlock XP]] and will unlock [[Backpack]] slots at exchange #1, #2, #4, #6, #8, #10, #13, #16, #19, #22, #25, #28, #31, #34, #37, #40, and #44; or in other words, after giving Tia 5, 10, 20, 30, 40, 50, 65, 80, 95, 110, 125, 140. 155, 170, 185, 200, and 220 Fairy Souls respectively. To obtain the boosts, the player must have at least five [[Fairy Souls]]. If the player has more than five souls, Tia will still only take five at a time.
### 9f™ Network Quest
After adding Tia the Fairy on player's [[Abiphones|Abiphone]], a [[9f™ Network|9f™ Operator Chip]] will be added into it. It will stay there as long as Tia is a contact. Wearing [[Fairy Armor]] allows the player to receive relays, where Tia will call the [[Player]] and tell the player where the [[9f™ Network]] that needs to be completed is.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Tia the Fairy: Welcome to the Fairy Pond, <player>! I am Tia. [NPC] Tia the Fairy: You may have noticed some strange orbs laying around the island. [NPC] Tia the Fairy: They are the souls of my fallen sisters. [NPC] Tia the Fairy: If you find any more during your travels, please bring them back to me!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ You have an Abiphone! [NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ That's super cool! [NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ Did you know the fairies helped the mages build the Abiphone's 9f™ network? [NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ But since so many of us are now missing, we're behind on maintenance! [NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ Come back wearing Fairy Armor and I can add a 9f™ Operator Chip to your Abiphone so you can help us out!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None; Wearing [[Fairy Armor]]|[NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ Sweet uniform! [NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ Okay I added 9f™ Operator Chip to your Abiphone plan. [NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ It will work on any Abiphone as long as I'm in your contacts list. [NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ We need to upgrade all of the 9f™ Network Relays spread across SkyBlock. [NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ Call me and I can tell you where the first relay is!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling; Insufficient contacts ([[Abiphones]]) for the; next Network Relay ([[9f™ Network]])|[NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ You need more contacts before you can deal with the next relay! [NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ Don't ask me why... [NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ I don't make the rules!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling; 1st Network Relay ([[9f™ Network]]) tip|[NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ Hey! It's you! On the Abiphone!!! [NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ The first relay is on a branch of the large tree on the north-east of the fairy pond. [NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ Make sure to wear Fairy Armor! [NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ I don't personally know what the maintenance procedure is, but I'm sure you can figure it out!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) 2nd Network Relay ([[9f™ Network]]) tip|[NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ Wow, you figured out the maintenance plan! [NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ That's really smart! [NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ The next relay is in the castle ruins!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) 3rd Network Relay ([[9f™ Network]]) tip|[NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ The next relay is in the Royal Palace within the Dwarven Mines. [NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ It's in a very high place to reverb the signal for the royal council. [NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ Although I don't think technology can help with their communication problems...|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) 4th Network Relay ([[9f™ Network]]) tip|[NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ The next relay is on the highest spike of The End. [NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ It's on the face which looks at the moon rising. [NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ Or the sun! If you're that kind of person...|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) 5th Network Relay ([[9f™ Network]]) tip|[NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ The next relay was placed by our consultant, Odger. [NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ He said "Travel too far home. Bye." after installing it and never called again. [NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ Maybe he can tell you where he put the relay?|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) 6th Network Relay ([[9f™ Network]]) tip|[NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ Scarleton itself has one of the most robust connection to the 9f™ Network. [NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ If you face the cathedral, there is a relay at the top of the left tower.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) 7th Network Relay ([[9f™ Network]]) tip|[NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ The next relay is on top of the shack next to the shady inn right here close to the pond. [NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ Usually we wouldn't have a relay serve so few people, but we were paid generously by anonymous contributors! [NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ So generously in fact that we also share some of the data from the 9f™ Network with one of the mayor candidates. [NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ But hey, let's keep this between us! [NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ They'll know if you tell anyone.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) 8th Network Relay ([[9f™ Network]]) tip|[NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ The next relay is on top of a statue in the dungeon hub. [NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ The one that doesn't have a fairy soul...|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) 9th Network Relay ([[9f™ Network]]) tip|[NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ The next relay is on top of the Auction House. [NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ It offers service to the whole village!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling; All Network Relays ([[9f™ Network]]) completed|[NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ Why is the tooth fairy so smart? [NPC] Tia the Fairy: ✆ Because she has wisdom teeth!|
|[[The Garden]] None; Missing Fairy Souls|[NPC] Tia the Fairy: You are currently missing [#] Fairy Souls. Better get to work!|
|[[The Garden]] None; All Fairy Souls collected|[NPC] Tia the Fairy: You collected every single Fairy Soul?!? Amazing!|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Tia the Fairy: That's exactly what I needed!|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Tia the Fairy: Alright, I won't bother you anymore.|
## Trivia
- Sometimes, Tia's head turns way faster than her body.
- Tia explains that when Fairies die, they become a [[Fairy Souls|Fairy Soul]], this is not the case in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]], but is demonstrated live in [[Stillgore Château]].
- [[Tal Ker]] claims, that Tia told him that all the fairies were killed, rather than dying a peaceful death.

Ticket Master is an [[NPC]] located in various [[Locations]] around the [[Dwarven Mines]] that allows players to easily travel around the [[Dwarven Mines]] at a cost. The cost of the minecart ride is calculated by multiplying the distance to the destination by 2x.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Purchasing a Ride|[NPC] Ticket Master: Hop on in and I'll get you going!|
|Purchasing a Ride|[NPC] Ticket Master: Hop in and we'll be off!|
|Purchasing a Ride|[NPC] Ticket Master: See that empty, weak metal box? Good! That's your cart, hop in!|
|Purchasing a Ride|[NPC] Ticket Master: Hold tight, it's going to be a bump ride!|
|Purchasing a Ride|[NPC] Ticket Master: Thanks for choosing Dwarven Mine Co. - we operate 24/7 across the whole mine! [NPC] Ticket Master: Now, please climb aboard before your cart leaves without you. [NPC] Ticket Master: Again.|
|Purchasing a Ride|[NPC] Ticket Master: Let's a go!|
|Purchasing a Ride|[NPC] Ticket Master: Ever been in one of these before? No? [NPC] Ticket Master: Well... [NPC] Ticket Master: I'm sure you'll be fine!|
|Purchasing a Ride|[NPC] Ticket Master: Last guy who was in this cart didn't feel too well so watch out he might have left a surprise in there.|
|Missed [[Minecart]]|[NPC] Ticket Master: Quickly now the Minecart is leaving! [NPC] Ticket Master: Oh no! It looks like you missed the Minecart!|
|Arriving|[NPC] Ticket Master: Welcome to the <location>!|
|Traveling to [[Hanging Court]]|[NPC] Ticket Master: Off to the court? I would tell you to talk to the King but I don't know who he is anymore...|
|Traveling to [[Hanging Court]]|[NPC] Ticket Master: Did you know the Court is almost as old as the throne room, it's got some lovely flower beds too!|
|Traveling to [[Gates to the Mines]]|[NPC] Ticket Master: See those statues? They are the former Kings, Bunumir Ironbeard and Skordous Pebblebeard, guarding the entrance to the mines.|
|Traveling to [[Aristocrat Passage]]|[NPC] Ticket Master: Aristocrat passage ey? Best of luck to you.|
|Traveling to [[Forge Basin]]|[NPC] Ticket Master: Did you know that the Forge is so big it has to have multiple forging stations.|
|Traveling to [[Goblin Burrows]]|[NPC] Ticket Master: You ever seen those Goblins? Some say they lay eggs. How weird.|
|Traveling to [[Goblin Burrows]]|[NPC] Ticket Master: Goblin's are not the only creatures that dwell here. Rumor has it dirt monsters linger in these parts too...|
|None|[NPC] Ticket Master: I once stumbled upon a hidden lake in these parts, Some unsavory characters living there robbed me of all my gold!|
|None|[NPC] Ticket Master: You came down the lift? I never thought you could get this far down! Someone should have called this place the Deeper Caverns!|
|None|[NPC] Ticket Master: Hey, if you find any Titanium down here let me know! I wanted to buy some of that stuff for a new pickaxe.|

# Ubik Von Neumann
Aliases: Split or Steal, Ubik
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Confirmations needed
[Ubik Von Neumann]
Type: [[NPC]]
[Locations]
Description: Lets the player play in their Three Options game.
Location: [[The Mountaintop]]
Coordinates: 21 107 54
Description: Lets the player play in their Averages game.
Location: [[The Mountaintop]]
Coordinates: 37.5 132 17
Description: Lets the player play in their Tit for Tat game.
Location: [[The Mountaintop]]
Coordinates: -19 135 41
Ubik Von Neumann is an [[NPC]] located in [[The Mountaintop]] in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Purpose
Ubik Von Neumann offers the player 3 minigames.
### Three Options
During Three Options, Ubik Von Neumann asks the player to choose one out of three boxes. After choosing one, they reveal an unchosen box and ask to keep the chosen box or switch. If the correct box was chosen, the player earns an [[IQ Point]]. The first time the player chooses to switch, Ubik Von Neumann will ask why. Telling them that it increases the odds of being correct grants an [[Enigma Souls|Enigma Soul]].
### Averages
Averages lasts for one SkyBlock Year. The player is asked to pick a number from 1 to 100. The closest 10% of participants to half of the average obtain a [[Free Will]] and [[Enigma Souls|Enigma Soul]]. Additionally, if a player picks a value at least 4x higher than the average, they earn 2 [[Free Will|Free Wills]].
If the player fails to win 5 times, then Ubik Von Neumann will grant them the [[Enigma Souls|Enigma Soul]] without a [[Free Will]].
### Tit For Tat
Each game of Tit For Tat can last between 5 to 9 rounds. If both player steal, they earn 1,000 [[Motes]] each. If both split, they earn 3,000 [[Motes]]. If one steals and the other splits, the stealer will earn 5,000 [[Motes]] while the other earns nothing. Upon copying the previous move of Ubik Von Neumann after choosing SPLIT for the first round, the player earns [[Ubik's Cube]] and an [[Enigma Souls|Enigma Soul]].

## Trivia
- Ubik von Neumann is likely named after mathematician John von Neumann, a pioneer of game theory, which directly inspires all three of Ubik's minigames. His first name may also reference the 1969 novel Ubik by Philip K. Dick.
  - The Tit For Tat minigame is based on one of the most famous thought experiments in game theory, the Prisoner's dilemma.
  - The Three Options minigame is based on the probability puzzle known as the Monty Hall problem.
  - The Averages minigame is inspired by the Keynesian Beauty Contest thought experiment, also known as the Guess 2/3 of the average game.
- Due to the Averages minigame considering the closest 10% of participants, it is possible to lose despite picking a number that other players won with.
  - In case of such a tie, the winners are determined based on unknown criteria (likely their Minecraft account UUID).[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
- 3 NPCs can be seen next to Ubik in the Split or Steal minigame, who are the rift version of the [[Dungeon Puzzle Rooms|Three Weirdos]].
  - Their names are also randomized.
  - The red colored NPC is often used for this minigame due to his playstyle being 100% SPLIT, letting players steal every round and get 25,000–45,000 [[Motes]] every game.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Dec 10, 2024|0.21|Added Ubik Von Neumann.|

# Udium
Categories: Non-player characters
[Udium]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Returns items that the player has transferred to the [[Rift Dimension]] through bugs and exploits.
Location: [[Wizard Tower]]
Coordinates: 46.5 101 100.5
Udium is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Wizard Tower]]. They return items the player transferred to the [[Rift Dimension]] through bugs and exploits.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[NPC] Udium: Magic is very dangerous. [NPC] Udium: One second everything is fine and the next, the world is ending. [NPC] Udium: I was playing with a Sculk Catalyst in a different world from the future... [NPC] Udium: And the sculk spread all over my body! [NPC] Udium: Even Alatar, a powerful wizard... [NPC] Udium: He just disappeared one day! [NPC] Udium: Poof! [NPC] Udium: No more Alatar!|
|Disallowed items being detected in the [[Rift Dimension]]|----------------------------------------------------- ⚠ UH OH! XX Some of your items did not belong here! Visit Udium in the Hub's Wizard Tower to get them (#) back! -----------------------------------------------------|
|Talking to Udium after disallowed items have been removed from The Rift|[NPC] Udium: Mmh... looks like you have some... weird items. [NPC] Udium: Here, I moved them to your stash for you!|
## Trivia
- Udium references the Sculk Catalyst in their dialogue, stating that "[they were] playing with [it] in a different world from the future...", probably referencing Minecraft version 1.19, and how SkyBlock was still centered on version 1.8.9 when the NPC was released.
- Udium also mentions [[Alatar]], an NPC found in the Rift, who "disappeared."
