# Ugo
Categories: Non-player characters, Crimson Isle
[Ugo]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Barbarian Faction Questline ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]) Join the Barbarian Faction ([[Quests]]); Recover the Dean's Research ([[Quests]]); Recover Sulphur ([[Quests]])
[Locations]
Description: Appears here at the start of the Barbarian Faction ([[Factions]]) Questline ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]).
Location: [[Crimson Isle]]
Coordinates: -508.5 98 -633.5
Description: Appears here during the Barbarian Faction ([[Factions]]) Questline ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]) to speak to [[Chief Scorn]].
Location: [[Chief's Hut]]
Coordinates: -587.5 115 -688.5
Description: Appears here during the Barbarian Faction ([[Factions]]) Questline ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]) after being kicked out of the [[Chief's Hut]].
Location: [[Dragontail]]
Coordinates: -612.5 107 -688.5
Description: Appears here during the Barbarian Faction ([[Factions]]) Questline ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]) for the player to complete tasks for them.
Location: [[Dragontail]]
Coordinates: -581.5 100 -684.5
Description: Appears here at the end of the [[Crimson Isle Main Questline]].
Location: [[The Wasteland]]
Coordinates: -369.5 117 -977.5
Description: Appears here at the end of the [[Crimson Isle Main Questline]] after defeating [[Kuudra]].
Location: [[Forgotten Skull]]
Coordinates: -370.5 113 -1036.5
Ugo is an [[NPC]] found in [[Dragontail]]. They are [[Chief Scorn]]'s son and a key figure in the [[Crimson Isle Main Questline|Barbarian Faction questline]].
## Quest
Ugo is found by the player and [[Elle]] in a little cave in the [[Burning Desert]] in the form of a pig. The player must feed them 1 [[Sulphur]] and escort Ugo and [[Elle]] to the [[Chief's Hut]]. Doing so will accept the player into the [[Factions|Barbarian Faction]].
Ugo and [[Elle]] will then ask the player to steal the Research from the [[Dean]] in the [[Cathedral]]. After successfully doing so, the player will gain [[Factions|+100 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation]], and Ugo will ask the player to complete a Test of Force, a Test of Stamina and a Test of Mastery, with a minimum rating of C.
Finally, Ugo will ask the player to kill a [[Mage Outlaw]] and a [[Magma Boss]]. Doing so will grant [[Factions|+150 ⚒️ Barbarian Reputation]], plus an additional 20 for killing the boss the first time that day.
The player will meet Ugo again after they complete [[Gris]]'s Quest, where they will meet Ugo, Elle and [[Captain Ahone]] outside the [[Forgotten Skull]] to defeat [[Kuudra]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Start of the Barbarian Faction ([[Factions]]) quest; (As a [[Pig]])|[NPC] Elle: You handled that quite well. [NPC] Elle: Corrupted Monsters rarely appear on the island, it usually means they consumed too much Sulphur. [NPC] ----: Oink [NPC] Elle: Did you hear that? [NPC] Elle: I think it was coming from here! [NPC] Elle: Let's go.|
|Before being given [[Sulphur]]; (As a [[Pig]])|[NPC] ----: Oink|
|Talking to Ugo after giving [[Sulphur]]|[NPC] Ugo: You saved me! [NPC] Ugo: Memory is failing me but I think I remember getting attacked. [NPC] Ugo: My leg got hurt and I had to hide here, the rest is blurry... [NPC] Elle: How could you leave Dragontail without Sulphur on you! [NPC] Ugo: I know, I was careless, I was on a mission for... [NPC] Ugo: SULPHUR! My father is going to KILL ME!! [NPC] Elle: Let's not exaggerate. [NPC] Ugo: You don't understand, my father is the Chief! [NPC] Elle: You were not exaggerating. [NPC] Ugo: Please help me, I have to present myself to him. [NPC] Elle: Of course. [NPC] Elle: I will shoulder you to the Chief's Hut. [NPC] Elle: <player> make sure monsters don't attack us on the way there.|
|Whilst [[Chief Scorn]] Scolds Ugo|[NPC] Chief Scorn: Ugo, where have you been? [NPC] Ugo: I- [NPC] Chief Scorn: You had ONE job! [NPC] Chief Scorn: 3 days later you come back with empty hands and a broken leg!? [NPC] Ugo: Well- [NPC] Chief Scorn: And you two, what are your names? [NPC] Elle: I'm- [NPC] Chief Scorn: Thank you for saving my rascal of a son. [NPC] Chief Scorn: Are you looking to join our ranks? [NPC] Elle: No but- [NPC] Chief Scorn: Perfect! [NPC] Chief Scorn: I welcome you both to the Barbarian Faction! [NPC] Chief Scorn: If you betray us you'll die. [NPC] Elle: I can't- [NPC] Chief Scorn: Enough talking! [NPC] Chief Scorn: Ugo, go back out there and finish the job! [NPC] Ugo: But- [NPC] Chief Scorn: Guards, escort them out of my Hut!|
|After being kicked out of the [[Chief's Hut]]|[NPC] Ugo: My leg really hurts. [NPC] Ugo: It was already quite bad before this fall but now... [NPC] Elle: Your father sure has some character. [NPC] Ugo: He's not that bad, you get used to it. [NPC] Elle: I don't know what he wants you to do out there, but we can't let you go back there with your injured leg. [NPC] Ugo: I'm afraid I have to. [NPC] Elle: Just tell us what you have to do and we'll do it for you. [NPC] Ugo: I can't tell you it's highly confidential. [NPC] Elle: I don't care, you can't go back out there with a broken leg. [NPC] Ugo: Sigh [NPC] Ugo: My father believes that the Mage Faction is working on some kind of powerful war instrument. [NPC] Ugo: We know they are hiding something inside their Cathedral. [NPC] Ugo: My job is to infiltrate their Cathedral and steal their war machine so we can study it. [NPC] Elle: Say no more, that's a perfect job for <player>. [NPC] Ugo: Why them? [NPC] Elle: Alright let's go.|
|Before stealing the research|[NPC] Ugo: Did you even agree to do this?|
|Before stealing the research|[NPC] Ugo: Thanks for helping out, and good luck!|
|After stealing the research|[NPC] Ugo: You actually did it, I can't thank you enough! [NPC] Ugo: Let's show it to my father. [NPC] Ugo: Oh and hum... [NPC] Ugo: Please don't mention that I didn't get it myself.|
|Presenting the research|[NPC] Chief Scorn: Ugo why are you limping? [NPC] Ugo: My le- [NPC] Chief Scorn: Have you found the evil wizard machine? [NPC] Ugo: Y- [NPC] Chief Scorn: Give it to the guards, we will investigate it. [NPC] Chief Scorn: Enough with the mages and their shenanigans. [NPC] Ugo: Alri- [NPC] Chief Scorn: The Town Board is filling up and you are late on your Training Ugo. [NPC] Chief Scorn: What have you been doing? [NPC] Ugo: You- [NPC] Chief Scorn: Hurry up, failure isn't an option.|
|After presenting research|[NPC] Ugo: Ugh [NPC] Ugo: I completely forgot about my Training. [NPC] Ugo: I have to go to the Town Board.|
|When the player is too slow when following Ugo|[NPC] Ugo: I'm right here, don't get lost.|
|When the player is too slow when following Ugo|[NPC] Ugo: Hey are you following?|
|When the player is too slow when following Ugo|[NPC] Ugo: What's taking so long?|
|When the player is too slow when following Ugo|[NPC] Ugo: Did you get lost?|
|When the player is too slow when following Ugo|[NPC] Ugo: Are you a snail?|
|When the player is too slow when following Ugo|[NPC] Ugo: Hello|
|When the player is too slow when following Ugo|[NPC] Ugo: Get a move on.|
|First interaction at the town board|[NPC] Ugo: This is our Town Board. [NPC] Ugo: Whenever someone needs something they put a request here and anyone can fulfill it. [NPC] Ugo: Not enough people are doing them so I usually have to do most requests myself. [NPC] Ugo: There are also Dojo requests, those are my training sessions... [NPC] Ugo: I don't think I can do my training with my bad leg. [NPC] Ugo: Master Tao has terrible sight, maybe you could go to the Dojo and pretend to be me? [NPC] Ugo: I'll wait for you here.|
|Before completing the [[Dojo]] tasks|[NPC] Ugo: The Dojo is on the opposite side of the island, south of Scarleton, the Mage city.|
|After completing the [[Dojo]] tasks|[NPC] Ugo: How was the training? Not too bad? [NPC] Ugo: I'm sorry you had to go through this. [NPC] Ugo: My father wants me to become the next Chief one day. [NPC] Ugo: I don't really have a choice. [NPC] Ugo: The Town Board missions get harder the higher your Reputation gets. [NPC] Ugo: If that's fine with you, I'll give you a couple of my missions, but they are quite tough. [NPC] Ugo: The first one requires eliminating a Mage Outlaw. [NPC] Ugo: They are technically Mages, but not part of the Mage Faction, they constantly cause problems. [NPC] Ugo: The one we need to eliminate is in the Courtyard, it's on the outskirts of Scarleton, towards the north.|
|Before killing the [[Mage Outlaw]]|[NPC] Ugo: Mage Outlaws often attack Piglins, the Wizards don't really want to deal with them so we have to do it ourself.|
|Before killing the [[Mage Outlaw]]|[NPC] Ugo: The Courtyard is a bit hidden, it is north-west of Scarleton, just outside of the city.|
|After killing the [[Mage Outlaw]]|[NPC] Ugo: You dealt with it? Great! [NPC] Ugo: You are quite competent, those Mages are usually quite strong, they also think very highly of themselves. [NPC] Ugo: The next mission is to kill the Magma Boss. [NPC] Ugo: He's an overgrown Magma Cube that keeps on growing infinitely. [NPC] Ugo: We have to defeat him every few weeks to keep it in check, otherwise it can grow out of control. [NPC] Ugo: It's very easy to find the Magma Boss, simply take any of the tunnels below the Volcano and descend until you reach the center.|
|Before killing the [[Magma Boss]]|[NPC] Ugo: Don't feel too bad about killing those Magma Cubes, they will be born again.|
|Before killing the [[Magma Boss]]|[NPC] Ugo: Once you find the large room below the Volcano, you'll be able to see it.|
|After killing the [[Magma Boss]]|[NPC] Ugo: I am glad you are back. [NPC] Ugo: You completed all my missions, I am very grateful. [NPC] Ugo: Now if you will excuse me, it's time for me to take care of my leg. [NPC] Ugo: See you around <player>.|
|Before meeting at the [[Forgotten Skull]]; (tasks completed)|[NPC] Ugo: Time to focus on myself.|
|Meeting outside the [[Forgotten Skull]]|[NPC] Elle: Captain Ahone, Ugo, this is a misunderstanding! [NPC] Elle: I saw what happened! I saw everything! [NPC] Elle: A monstruous[sic] creature was carrying the Sulphur, mountains of Sulphur! [NPC] Elle: The monster had giant tentacles, I have never seen anything like it before in my life! [NPC] Captain Ahone: Elle of the Nether, do not attempt to trick us. [NPC] Captain Ahone: Your motives are noble, but time for diplomacy is over. [NPC] Captain Ahone: Barbarians, return our Sulphur at once, or face the consequences of your actions. [NPC] Ugo: WE stole YOUR Sulphur?! You wizards have some nerves! [NPC] Ugo: We came here following the trails of the Sulphur that YOU robbed! [NPC] Elle: ENOUGH! [NPC] Elle: You were BOTH robbed, isn't it obvious?! [NPC] Elle: The culprit is inside, and it is not Human nor Piglin. [NPC] Elle: I have no idea what it is, but I have to warn you, that fiend is immensely powerful. [NPC] Elle: Now if you wish to retrieve your Sulphur, follow me inside. [NPC] Captain Ahone: This giant tentacle monster story is real? [NPC] Captain Ahone: I have trouble believing it, but in any case we have to get to the bottom of this. [NPC] Ugo: I agree, let's get inside.|
|Right after defeating [[Kuudra]] for the first time|[NPC] Elle: What a fight! [NPC] Ugo: And we got all the Sulphur back! [NPC] Ugo: But what exactly was that monster? [NPC] Captain Ahone: No idea, such a titan appearing out of nowhere isn't a good sign. [NPC] Captain Ahone: Especially if it's after our Sulphur. [NPC] Ugo: We clearly hurt it real bad, but for all we know it's still alive. [NPC] Captain Ahone: Barbarians, I would like to apologise for accusing you. You fought well out there. [NPC] Ugo: No hard feelings, I also judged you too quickly. [NPC] Ugo: You too <player>, you and your friends fought bravely, we couldn't have done it without you [NPC] Captain Ahone: I have to report to the Queen, there is no time to waste. [NPC] Ugo: I'll also alert our Chief. Time to go. [NPC] Elle: Is anyone going to thank me?|
|Speaking to [[Chief Scorn]] after defeating [[Kuudra]]|[NPC] Chief Scorn: You finally retrieved the Sulphur. [NPC] Chief Scorn: What took you so long? [NPC] Ugo: A gian- [NPC] Chief Scorn: You even needed the help of those adventurers. [NPC] Chief Scorn: Do you have no shame? [NPC] Ugo: I didn't- [NPC] Chief Scorn: Look at the time. You are late to your training. [NPC] Chief Scorn: Guards, escort my son out of the hut. [NPC] Chief Scorn: <player> I am in your debt. [NPC] Chief Scorn: I cherish Ugo more than anything else in this world. [NPC] Chief Scorn: A father could not be prouder of his son. [NPC] Chief Scorn: My mind is at peace knowing you are by his side. [NPC] Chief Scorn: More importantly, I am glad he finally met someone he can consider his friend. [NPC] Chief Scorn: I taught him everything I could, but there are things only friends can teach. [NPC] Chief Scorn: I won't hold you any longer. [NPC] Chief Scorn: Good luck in your adventures.|

# Unhinged Kloon
Aliases: Kloon, Server Room, Server room
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Unhinged Kloon]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Tasks the player with hacking terminals, rewarding the [[Defective Monitor]] when complete.
Location: [[West Village]]
Coordinates: -56.5 79 -13.71875
Unhinged Kloon is an [[NPC]] located in the [[West Village]]. They ask the player to hack 8 terminals with the [[Retro-Encabulating Visor]] and change their colors.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Kloon: Ghasts produce chemtrails! [NPC] Kloon: We never reached the end! [NPC] Kloon: Minecraft worlds are round! [NPC] Kloon: Biggest of all, Big Mayor is always watching!!! Select an option: [You okay?]|
|Selecting [You Okay?]|[NPC] Kloon: They hacked me, I'm telling you! [NPC] Kloon: All the cables are gray now! [NPC] Kloon: I don't want gray, I hate gray! Select an option: [Cables?]|
|Selecting [Cables?]|[NPC] Kloon: Take this Visor ([[Retro-Encabulating Visor]]), follow the cables and hack them back!!!|
|Without owning the [[Retro-Encabulating Visor]]|[NPC] Kloon: Did you lose my visor? [NPC] Kloon: No problem! I got tons of them!|
|After obtaining the [[Retro-Encabulating Visor]]|[NPC] Kloon: Quick!|
|After obtaining the [[Retro-Encabulating Visor]] and if you haven't hacked all the terminals|[NPC] Kloon: Use the Visor. [NPC] Kloon: Follow the cables real close. [NPC] Kloon: Hack the terminals. [NPC] Kloon: Pick the right colors. [NPC] Kloon: It's not so big brain.|
|If you hacked all the terminals, but aren't in a rainbow facing Kloon|[NPC] Kloon: I see you've hacked all the terminals, that's good... [NPC] Kloon: But you have to set them to the proper colors so I can hack their nexus!!!|
|If the cables are the right colors|[NPC] Kloon: Wow [player], you did it! [NPC] Kloon: Now that their own network is working against them, I can start working on infiltrating Big Mayor. Select an option: [What's my reward?]|
|Selecting [What's my reward]|[NPC] Kloon: Shouldn't the reward of video games be the fun you've had playing them? [NPC] Kloon: Alright fine... [NPC] Kloon: Take this old thing.|
|After claiming the [[Defective Monitor]]|[NPC] Kloon: Thank you for stopping Big Mayor from watching my every moves. [NPC] Kloon: Do you need some items again?|
## Trivia
- Unhinged Kloon is a reference to 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] Minikloon, a former 12[[Ranks|[ADMIN]]]. Their skin is a black version of Minikloon's skin.
- Unhinged Kloon's dialogue references conspiracy theories about chemtrails, the Moon landings, the shape of the Earth, and Big Brother.

[SkyBlock Prototype]

|Selecting [NO]|[NPC] Velyna: I knew you would say that. [NPC] Velyna: You just wanted to see what I would say, and then ask again. [NPC] Velyna: How predictable. [NPC] Velyna: In any case, I'll read your future.|

|After Reading Future; Player has not Defeated [[Kuudra]]|[NPC] Velyna: I also saw some pretty embarrassing stuff...|
|After Reading Future; Player has not Defeated [[Kuudra]]|[NPC] Velyna: Not everyone wants to see their future, but they should.|

# Violetta
Categories: Non-player characters
[Violetta]
Type: [[NPC]]
[Locations]

Violetta is an [[NPC]] who first appears in the [[Barry Center]] in the [[Rift Dimension]]. They start out as a protestor against [[Barry]], but after the player handles their complaints, they move to [[Barry HQ]] and become a worker.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Before first interaction with [[Barry]]|[NPC] Violetta: Barry is hiding from us! [NPC] Violetta: He doesn't want to face reality!|
|Before first interaction with [[Barry]]|[NPC] Violetta: Barry is a terrible leader! [NPC] Violetta: He doesn't want to hear us out!|
|Before first interaction with [[Barry]]|[NPC] Violetta: Someone promised donuts when coming to this protest. [NPC] Violetta: And I don't see ANY DONUT! [NPC] Violetta: Screw Barry!|
|After [[Barry]] says to see protestors|[NPC] Violetta: My math homework is way too hard! [NPC] Violetta: Prime Minister Barry should do my homework!!! I should tell Barry about Violetta's complaints...|
|After telling Barry about Violetta's complaints|[NPC] Violetta: My homework asks... [NPC] Violetta: If Mary is 10 years old and Jamie is 25. [NPC] Violetta: And considering that Mary's time flows 2 and a half times faster than Jamie's... [NPC] Violetta: In how long will Mary be the same age as Jamie? Select the appropriate Barry rhetoric: ① [In 15 years.] ② [In 12.5 years.] ③ [In 10 years.]|
|Selecting [In 15 years.]|[NPC] Violetta: In 15 years? Hold on... [NPC] Violetta: No, that really doesn't work. [NPC] Violetta: Why don't you ask Barry instead of trying to think for yourself.|
|Selecting [In 12.5 years.]|[NPC] Violetta: That's why I thought at first, but my teacher told me I'm stupid.|
|Selecting [In 10 years.]|[NPC] Violetta: Lemme see... In 10 years they will both be 35 years old. [NPC] Violetta: Incredible! How did you figure it out?? [NPC] Violetta: Wait actually, I prefer choosing ignorance.|
|In [[Barry HQ]]|[NPC] Violetta: A Prime Minister who can help with your homework? [NPC] Violetta: Truly someone deserving of their title!|

- While in [[Barry Center]], Violetta and the other protestors have dialogue above their heads, such as "Barry bad!", "Go away Barry!", "Barry is trash.", "Go away Barry!", "Barry boooo!", and "Barry sucks!"

# Weird Sailor
Categories: Non-player characters
[Weird Sailor]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Allows the player to fish up the [[Wet Napkin]] under certain conditions.
Location: [[Kuudra's Hollow]]
Coordinates: -131 93 -177
The Weird Sailor is an [[NPC]] located in [[Kuudra's Hollow]].
## Purpose
The Weird Sailor appears in [[Kuudra's Hollow]] after defeating [[Kuudra]] and within 30 minutes of donating 1,000,000 [[Coins]] to the [[Kuudra Believer]].
The player can obtain the [[Wet Napkin]] by fishing next to them with a Lava [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]] and [[Ice Bait]] while wearing an [[Elegant Tuxedo]] and a [[Bonzo's Mask]] with the [[Respiration (Enchantment)|Respiration I]] [[Enchantments|Enchantment]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Line Requirement|Dialogue|
|-|-|-|
|None|None|[NPC] Weird Sailor: hi|
|None|Wearing an [[Elegant Tuxedo]]|[NPC] Weird Sailor: you have elegant taste in fashion|
|None|None|[NPC] Weird Sailor: u want to know a secret? [NPC] Weird Sailor: ... [NPC] Weird Sailor: i can hold my breath under lava for 15 seconds!|
|None|Wearing a Helmet ([[Helmets]]) with Respiration I ([[Respiration (Enchantment)]])|[NPC] Weird Sailor: seems like u can too!|
|None|None|[NPC] Weird Sailor: ... [NPC] Weird Sailor: i heard a legend there was a big fish in these waters [NPC] Weird Sailor: mayb u could fish it up?|
|None|Holding [[Ice Bait]]|[NPC] Weird Sailor: try using dat bait u hav|
|None|None|[NPC] Weird Sailor: ... [NPC] Weird Sailor: anyway... i have a train to catch [NPC] Weird Sailor: im goin 2 da circus|
|None|Wearing a [[Bonzo's Mask]]|[NPC] Weird Sailor: u can join me if u like|
|None|None|[NPC] Weird Sailor: bai (⌐■_■)/|
## Trivia
- The Weird Sailor sits in a [[Minecart]] floating on [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Lava]].
- Assuming the 'big fish' mentioned in the Weird Sailor's dialogue is [[Kuudra]]; it is the second [[NPC]] that references trying to fish up [[Kuudra]], the first being [[Elle]].
- Any reference to [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]] in the Weird Sailors dialogue is actually [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Lava]].
- The '15 seconds' refers to the time extension of [[Respiration (Enchantment)|Respiration I]].
- The 'circus' mentioned in the Weird Sailor's dialogue could be related to [[Bonzo]]'s arena.
  - This is enforced by the fact that a [[Bonzo's Mask]] is required to trigger a line of dialogue.
- It is one of three [[NPC|NPCs]] that speak in internet slang, the others being [[Fetchur]] and [[Fossil Muncher]].
- The location of the Weird Sailor is blocked behind the barriers before and during the fight of Kuudra.
## History

|Sep 17, 2022|The Weird Sailor was discovered.|
|Oct 11, 2022|Added additional dialogue lines for specific conditions.|
|Oct 11, 2022|The [[Wet Napkin]] was discovered.|
|Oct 19, 2022|0.16|Moved the Weird Sailor from -131, 53, -117 ([[Coordinates]]) to -131, 92, -178 ([[Coordinates]]).|
|Oct 19, 2022|0.16|Added barriers around the Weird Sailor that automatically get destroyed when [[Kuudra]] is defeated and someone in the run is blessed by the [[Kuudra Believer]].|

# Wizard (Catacombs)
Categories: Non-player characters, Catacombs, Dungeons
[Wizard]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Crystal Ball
Description: Tasks the player with obtaining the [[Wizard's Crystal]].
Location: The Catacombs ([[Catacombs]])
The Wizard is an [[NPC]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]] (4)-[[The Catacombs - Floor VII|Floor VII]] (7) that can be found in a room with books. The first time you interact with him in the run, he will tell you that he has lost his [[Wizard's Crystal|Crystal Ball]] and that you have to retrieve it for him. The Crystal is located in a hidden lava cavern. After you have given his Crystal Ball back to him, he will grant you a [[Dungeon Blessing|Blessing of Wisdom]].
## Dialogue
- Grammar and/or punctuation mistakes are intentional, as they appear so in the dialogue in-game.
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Quest|[NPC] Wizard: Mi Wizard, mi lost mi crystal ball [NPC] Wizard: If you find mi crystal, can you give it back to me lady|
|Completing Quest|[NPC] Wizard: Oh my lovely crystal ball, mi so happy You deserve a reward young gobelin [NPC] Wizard: Granted your team a Blessing of Wisdom I|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|During Quest|[NPC] Wizard: Just old wizard y'know|
|During Quest|[NPC] Wizard: Mi don't know what I'd do with no crystal ball|
|During Quest|[NPC] Wizard: Are you really a lady? You ain't trying to play tricks with mi I hope.|
|During Quest|[NPC] Wizard: You find mi crystal ball?|
## Gallery
- The Wizard
- The location of the Wizard's Crystal
## Trivia
- The Wizard's dialogues have some intentional grammar and spelling mistakes.
  - They say "mi" instead of "my" and "i'm".
  - They also lack some commas.
- A gobelin is a French tapestry, although it is possible that the Wizard actually meant "goblin" instead of "gobelin".
- Turning in the [[Wizard's Crystal]] does not count as one of the four dungeon secrets in the room he spawns in.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Nov 17, 2020|0.9.13|Added Wizard.|

# Wizardman
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, More info needed
[Wizardman]
Type: [[NPC]]
[Locations]
Description: Talks about how they can be called by typing "Help Wizardman!" in chat.
Location: Wizard Tower ([[Wizard Tower (Rift)]])
Coordinates: -45 90 69.5
Description: Appears here during [[Detective Amog]]'s reverse-murder quest.
Location: [[Village Plaza]]
Coordinates: -51.5 68 -52.5
Description: Appears here after typing "Help Wizardman!" in chat, and lets the player earn various items that lead to becoming Wizardman.
Location: [[Wizardman Bureau]]
Coordinates: -222.5 19 -88.5
Description: Appears here when participating in the Temporal Race.
Location: [[Trial Grounds]]
Coordinates: 43 132 22
Wizardman is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Wizard Tower (Rift)|Wizard Tower]]. He also appears during [[Detective Amog]]'s reverse-murder quest and in the [[Wizardman Bureau]] after saying Help Wizardman! in chat.
## Quests
Wizardman first appears in the [[Wizard Tower (Rift)|Wizard Tower]], where he instructs the player to say "Help Wizardman!" in chat to receive his help later on.
### Reverse Murder
Towards the end of [[Detective Amog]]'s reverse-murder quest, he tasks the player with shouting "Help Wizardman!". Doing so will make Wizardman appear and help destroy the wall that [[Ramero]] put up around the [[Murder House]].
### Trials
Upon saying "Help Wizardman!" in the [[Wizardman Bureau]], Wizardman will appear and allow the player to compete with him in 3 different trials. Each trial can be attempted an unlimited number of times.
#### Temporal Race
Face Wizardman in a parkour trial.
The player must race against Wizardman in parkour. During the race, rewind buttons appear long the track. Punching a rewind button rewinds Wizardman's location to where they were several seconds ago. Completing the trial rewards 1 [[Ugly Boots Fragment]].
#### Wizard Brawl
Face Wizardman in a combat trial.
The player is teleported to the [[Wizard Brawl]] and has to defeat Wizardman in combat. They are given a [[Staff of the Rising Moon]] to damage him with. Wizardman has 12 [[Hearts]] during the trial. The player can also press the jump button twice to fly.
Once the player is ready, they can use the [[Staff of the Rising Moon]] damage Wizardman. Hitting Wizardman with a projectile removes 1 [[Hearts]] from him. Falling into lava or getting hit by a projectile from Wizardman deals 1 [[Hearts]] to the player. Completing the trial rewards 1 [[W Logo Fragment]].
#### Quad Link Legacy
Face Wizardman in an epic battle of wits.
The player must play Connect Four against Wizardman. Completing the trial rewards 1 [[Tight Pants Fragment]].
#### Wizard Face Reward
Using the rewards from the trials, the player can craft [[Ugly Boots]], [[Wizardman Leggings]], and [[W Logo]]. Wearing these while speaking to Wizardman awards the player with a [[Wizard Face]].
By speaking to Wizardman while wearing the face, a cutscene triggers, which ends with the player at the top of [[The Mountaintop]] next to [[Defunct Wizardman]].

[NPC] Wizardman: All you have to do is scream "Help Wizardman!" and I'll come save you if I'm not too busy. [NPC] Wizardman: Ah. [NPC] Wizardman: Sorry young companion, someone needs me! [NPC] Wizardman: I've got to skedaddle.|

|Selecting [Let's race again.]|[NPC] Wizardman: Very well.|
|Selecting [I would like to do another trial.]|[NPC] Wizardman: Very well. WOOSH!|

|Once Wizardman loses 8 [[Hearts]]|[NPC] Wizardman: You've pushed me to the brink. [NPC] Wizardman: But don't underestimate a Wizardman's last stand.|
|Once Wizardman loses 8 [[Hearts]]|[NPC] Wizardman: It seems I may have met my match.|

[NPC] Wizardman: For your victory, you've earned a Fragment of my armor. [NPC] Wizardman: Keep it safe, for it is a piece of my power.|

|Losing in Wizard Brawl|[NPC] Wizardman: Ah, little fellow, it seems this isn't your moment. [NPC] Wizardman: Return when the gaps in your skill are filled.|

### Quad Link Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Choosing to play Quad Link Legacy for the first time|[More Info Needed]|
|Choosing to play Quad Link Legacy|[NPC] Wizardman: Ah, the Quad Link Legacy! A personal favorite of mine. [NPC] Wizardman: Prepare yourself for a battle of wits. [NPC] Wizardman: Would you like to review the rules? Select an option: [Yes please!] [No thanks.] [No, please don't ask me again.]|
|Selecting Yes please!|[NPC] Wizardman: Each turn, we will choose a column to drop a colored token into the lowest open slot. [NPC] Wizardman: The first to align four tokens of their color in a row claims victory! [NPC] Wizardman: Now that you know the rules, let's begin.|
|Selecting [No thanks.]|[NPC] Wizardman: Very well, let's begin then.|
|Selecting [No, please don't ask me again.]|[NPC] Wizardman: Very well. I will not ask you again.|
|Randomly during a game of Quad Link Legacy|[NPC] Wizardman: What could <playername> be thinking right now?|
|Randomly during a game of Quad Link Legacy|[NPC] Wizardman: I'm thinking two steps ahead... hmmm...|
|Randomly during a game of Quad Link Legacy|[NPC] Wizardman: Hmmmm, what can I play to win?|
|Randomly during a game of Quad Link Legacy|[NPC] Wizardman: Do not rush the almighty Wizardman.|
|Winning in Quad Link Legacy|[NPC] Wizardman: Well played! [NPC] Wizardman: It seems I've underestimated your capacity for foresight and planning. [NPC] Wizardman: For your victory, you've earned a Fragment of my armor. [NPC] Wizardman: Keep it safe, for it is a piece of my power.|
|Leaving mid-game|[NPC] Wizardman: We will complete our match one day, little one.|
|Losing in Quad Link Legacy|[NPC] Wizardman: Ah, it seems the depths of wisdom still elude you. Wizardman defeated you in Quad Link Legacy! [NPC] Wizardman: Return when the fogs of ignorance have lifted.|
|Draw|[NPC] Wizardman: That was a close one! The Quad Link Legacy game with Wizardman ended in a draw. [NPC] Wizardman: Unfortunately you didn't beat me.|
|After the Quad Link Legacy|[NPC] Wizardman: Would you care to challenge me again, or would you prefer to attempt a different Trial? Select an option: [Let's brawl again.] [I would like to do another trial.]|
|Selecting [Let's brawl again.]|[NPC] Wizardman: Very well. Let's put your wits to the test.|
|Selecting [I would like to do another trial.]|[NPC] Wizardman: Very well.|
## Trivia
- Aliases for Help Wizardman! include Help Wizardman and other combinations with varying capitalized letters.
- Wizardman disappears forever in the player's current lobby once they trigger Wizardman's first interaction dialogue. They will re-appear in a new lobby, however.
- During the final dialogue, after wearing the [[Wizard Face]] and talking to the Wizardman, a cutscene plays, where the [[Player]] can be seen performing the activities mentioned by Wizardman.
  - Once the dialogue and cutscene come to end, the player finds themselves near the top of the [[The Mountaintop]], next to [[Defunct Wizardman]] and is granted the [[Achievements|"Top of the Wizard food chain" Achievement]].

# Xalx
Aliases: Xalax, Xlax
Categories: Non-player characters, Crystal Hollows
[Xalx]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Gives the player [[Mysterious Meat]].
Location: [[Goblin Holdout]]
Xalx is an [[NPC]] that can be found in the [[Goblin Holdout]] in the [[Crystal Hollows]]. They grant the player [[Mysterious Meat]], as well granting [[Mysterious Crop]] as a visitor in [[The Garden]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Guranteed Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[NPC] Xalx: Hello traveler! Would you like to try my new recipe? [NPC] Xalx: This meat is a special blend of well... everything.|
|Eating [[Mysterious Meat]]|[NPC] Xalx: Ahh you stinky humans must have weak stomach. Maybe you will like my Zog stew? [NPC] Xalx: OH well, maybe next time.|
|Interacting with an [[Unfulfilled Jerryseed]] and [[Mysterious Crop]]|[NPC] Xalx: Ooooh, what's this? You bring Xalx funny little seed and strange crop? [NPC] Xalx: Smells... weird. But not bad weird! Good weird! Like stew left in sun for three days. [NPC] Xalx: Hmmm... something missing. Needs... more! More flavor! More stink! [NPC] Xalx: Here, take this back. Let it... marinate. Yes, marinate! Then it be ready.|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Xalx: Hello gardener, I've been looking for some sugar to grow my new crop. Wanna try it?|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Xalx: Darn humans, you don't understand how to use things...|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Xalx: Smart choice.|
|[[The Garden]] Speaking as a Guest ([[Guesting]])|[NPC] Xalx: Hmm... I haven't seen you around here before. Do you work here?|
## Trivia
- Xalx's skin is a slightly modified [[Goblin]] skin.
- The use of Xalx's [[Mysterious Meat]] is currently unknown.
- Xalx could be a reference to Xiangling in Genshin Impact. Xiangling is a chef with similar dialogue to Xalx. The use of the term "traveler" in the dialogue is common in both characters.

# Yoshua

Yoshua is an [[NPC]] located in the [[West Village]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[NPC] Yoshua: Do you ever feel like we're just programmed to do certain things in life? [NPC] Skylark: I used to, because it seems like everything you CAN do is pre-determined, like a computer program. [NPC] Skylark: Like an all-powerful entity created this world and everything that can exist in it. [NPC] Yoshua: What made you change your mind? [NPC] Skylark: Well, think of it like a video game. # Event Shop
Categories: Non-SkyBlock specific mechanics
[Event Shop]
Event Shop is a server feature on [[Hypixel]] and is not specific to one minigame. More information can be found on Hypixel Wiki at Event Shop.
The Event Shop is a Hypixel shop that can be accessed during seasonal events to purchase cosmetics for various gamemodes, including [[SkyBlock]].
## Mechanics
The Event Shop can be accessed in the main Hypixel lobby by the Event Guide during Easter, Summer, Halloween, and the Holidays. Cosmetics cost Event Silver, which is a network-wide currency gained by earning event levels from playing games, with each level requiring 100 event XP, up to level 100.
Every event level gained grants Event Silver, with higher [[Ranks]] granting more silver, as well as more XP to level up faster:
|Rank|XP Multiplier|Silver Per Level|
|-|-|-|
|1Default ([[Ranks]])|1x|10|

# Hypixel
Aliases: Hypixel Network
Categories: Non-SkyBlock specific mechanics, Pages with UIs
Hypixel is a server feature on Hypixel and is not specific to one minigame. More information can be found on Hypixel Wiki at Hypixel Network.
The Hypixel Network, alternatively referred to as the Hypixel Server or simply Hypixel, is a Minecraft minigame server founded by Simon "Hypixel" Collins-Laflamme and Philippe Touchette. Currently, it is only available in the Java Edition of Minecraft and was formerly available in Pocket Edition under the codename BladeStorm. The Hypixel server is where [[SkyBlock|Hypixel SkyBlock]] is hosted.
## History
The server was originally created in 2012 and released to the public on April 13, 2013. It was originally made because Simon wanted to show off the maps he made, but later on, minigames like Hypixel SkyBlock were added into the server and that is where the server heavily grew in popularity.
In 2015, it was revealed that the server cost around $100,000 per month to maintain. At the time, the server had around 16,000 concurrent players. By January 2021, the server regularly reached over 100,000 players online.
On December 21, 2016, Hypixel reached a total of 10 million unique players, and as of September 15, 2015, Hypixel attracted around 1.9 million players every month.
Around April 2018, Hypixel began to use DDoS protection after a denial of service attack against the server by Mirai.

# Ranks
Aliases: Rank
Categories: Non-SkyBlock specific mechanics, Mechanics
[Ranks]
Ranks are a server feature on [[Hypixel]] and are not specific to one minigame. More information can be found on Hypixel Wiki at Ranks.
Ranks are features on Hypixel that give various rewards across the Hypixel network, including some SkyBlock-specific perks.
## Ranks
### Purchasable Ranks
The player can purchase a number of ranks through the Hypixel Store using real-life money.
The prices below exclude taxes. Some regions (e.g. Europe) add tax to the price in the listing, while others (e.g. United States) add tax at checkout.
|Rank|Benefits|Pricing|
|-|-|-|
|2[VIP]|Game replays (/games) command No chat cooldown Rank-specific Cosmetics Event Silver Multiplier: 1.5x Housing House Slots: 2 Priority Queue Armor Wardrobe ([[Wardrobe]]) Slots: 6 [[Equipment Wardrobe]] Slots: 4 [[Loadouts]] Slots: 6 SkyBlock Quick Crafting Access Seasonal SkyBlock Salesman Extended Replay Access: 1 day Flying in lobbies Click Effects Housing Mailboxes Ability to gift ranks|$6.99 USD|
|3[VIP+]|All 2[VIP] perks Rank-specific Cosmetics Event Silver Multiplier: 2x SkyBlock Wardrobe ([[Wardrobe]]) Slots: 10 [[Equipment Wardrobe]] Slots: 5 [[Loadouts]] Slots: 10 Extended Replay Access: 2 days Create Guilds|$14.99 USD|
|4[MVP]|All 3[VIP+] perks Rank-specific Cosmetics Event Silver Multiplier: 2.5x Housing House Slots: 3 SkyBlock Wardrobe ([[Wardrobe]]) Slots: 14 [[Equipment Wardrobe]] Slots: 7 [[Loadouts]] Slots: 14 Extended Replay Access: 3 days|$29.99 USD|
|5[MVP+]|All 4[MVP] perks Rank-specific Cosmetics Event Silver Multiplier: 3x SkyBlock Wardrobe ([[Wardrobe]]) Slots: 18 [[Equipment Wardrobe]] Slots: 9 [[Loadouts]] Slots: 18 Extended Replay Access: 4 days Change your plus ("+") color Game Map Selectors Achievements Tracking SkyBlock EPIC ([[Rarity]]) Warp Scrolls ([[Travel Scrolls]]) Particle Packs Ride & Control Lobby Pets|$44.99 USD|
|6[MVP++]|All 5[MVP+] perks Rank-specific Cosmetics Event Silver Multiplier: 3.5x Statuses Private Games (/party private) Command Nickname (/nick) Command Golden Guild Tag Exclusive In-Game Emojis Ability to change color overlay /stream Command Golden "GG" at the end of games 500 [[SkyBlock Gems]] every month|$7.99 USD/30 days; $21.99 USD/90 days; $39.99 USD/180 days; $70.99 USD/1 year|
### Other Ranks
These ranks are not available for purchase and are only given to qualified players.
|Rank|Obtaining|
|-|-|
|55[ዞ]|All Hypixel staff, including the owners, have this rank.|
|7[YOUTUBE]|This rank is given to members of Hypixel's creator program.|
|8[PIG+++]|This rank is exclusive to the late 8[PIG+++] Technoblade, and it was gifted personally by 55[ዞ] hypixel after Technoblade won numerous Minecraft Mondays.|
|54[INNIT]|This rank is exclusive to 54[INNIT] TommyInnit, and it was gifted personally by 55[ዞ] hypixel after TommyInnit hit 15,000,000 subscribers on YouTube.|
|9[MOJANG]|This rank is exclusive to employees at Mojang Studios.|
|10[EVENTS]|This rank is exclusive to 10[EVENTS] HypixelEvents, an account dedicated to official events run by Hypixel staff.|
|14[MCP]|This rank is exclusive to 14[MCP] MasterControl, an account owned by developers.|

# Defend Jerry's Workshop
Aliases: Gift Attack, Liquid Hot Magma, Snow Cannon
Categories: Outdated pages, Pages with broken file links, Confirmations needed, Events, Season of Jerry
Jerry's Workshop Events
Jerry's Workshop Opens
:
Countdown:
Dates and times based on browser time.
Season of Jerry
:
Countdown:
Dates and times based on browser time.
Defend Jerry's Workshop is an event that is part of the [[Season of Jerry|Season of Jerry Event]]. It happens at the end of the 23rd of Late Winter on [[Jerry's Workshop]].
## Objective
The players' objective is to defeat Magma Cubes emerging from [[Mount Jerry]] before they reach the Gift Piles located in the town. There are 5 waves in total.
### Damage
To deal damage to the Magma Cubes during the event, a [[Frosty the Snow Cannon]], [[Frosty the Snow Blaster]] or [[Frosty the Howitzer]] must be used to shoot Snowballs at them. Each Snowball that hits a Magma Cube will deduct 1 from its [[Health]].
Mountable Snow Cannons can be used to damage Liquid Hot Magmas, but not Magma Cubes.
The amount of damage the player has dealt is visible in the scoreboard.
## Mechanics
### Mount Jerry eruption
Mount Jerry
Mount Jerry (Erupted)
The eruption (sped up by 8×)
At the start of the event, Mount Jerry will begin erupting. Snow will melt and ice will deplete.
### Power-Ups
Power-Ups spawn around the village in random intervals and last 80 seconds when picked up.
|Power-Up|Effects|
|-|-|
|Homing Snowballs|The snowballs fired from your Snow Cannon now home towards Liquid Hot Magmas and Magma Cube Bosses|
|Double Up|The snowballs are fired from your Snow Cannon at 2× the rate.|
|Strongarm|Snowballs travel much farther and deal double damage.|
### Snow Cannons
Snow Cannons can be mounted and used to shoot snowballs. Only one player can mount a snow cannon at a time, and when a player mounts one, it will trigger a message, which will be broadcast to the entire lobby. Two are on top of buildings, and two at the gate of the village in Jerry's Workshop.
When fired, they deal 10 [[Damage]] to mobs in a 10x10 area where the large snowball lands. The Snow Cannon cannot damage Magma Cubes.
Snow Cannon
### Mobs
#### Magma Cubes
Lucky 7 (700 [[Health]] )
There are 5 waves with 10 different sizes/types of Magma Cube mini-bosses spawning on top of [[Mount Jerry]] during the eruption. They will slowly make their way down the mountain to destroy the Gift Piles.
|Magma Cube Name|[[Health]]|
|-|-|
|Cubie|100|
|Maggie|200|
|Cubert|300|
|Cübe|400|
|Cubette|500|
|Magmalene|600|
|Lucky 7|700|
|8ball|800|
|Mega Cube|900|
|Super Cube|900|
The damage dealt by the Magma Cubes will progressively increase for each new size of Magma Cube and every new wave.
#### Liquid Hot Magma
They have 20 [[Health]] , with great speed and hold Magma Creams or Fire Charges. They usually shoot fireballs for their main attack, but will occasionally switch to a bow to deal [[Damage]] and [[Knockback]]. Can be damaged with normal weapons.
- Liquid Hot Magma
- Liquid Hot Magma
- Liquid Hot Magma
### Rewards
At the end of each wave, the number of gifts you will receive in all the Gift Piles will be displayed along with some other statistics such as:
- Snowballs Hit
- Snowballs Thrown
- Snowballs Accuracy
- Total Damage
  - Your # Position
- Magma Cube Damage
Go to each Gift Pile to receive the gifts you earned.
#### Gift Piles
One of the Gift Piles.
There are 5 Gift Piles scattered below Mount Jerry, forming a path labeled Ⓐ (A), Ⓑ (B), Ⓒ (C), Ⓓ (D), and Ⓔ (E). Look for the Green beacon beam to locate them. Your rewards will depend on how many Gift Piles are preserved at the end of each wave.
If the gift piles are all destroyed, Gary uses his snow machine and rains snow on the monsters.
Refer to the status bar on the top of your screen to view the overall HP of your Gift Piles along with their individual status:
- Green: Not destroyed
- Yellow: Targeting
- White/Red: Partially destroyed
- Ⓧ (X): Destroyed
Possible rewards given from the Gift Pile are:
This section needs to be reworked as its content is outdated. You can help the wiki by editing this page and reworking it.
This article was last edited by AAAHHHHAHHAAHAAH (contribs|logs|block) on 12:02, October 14 2026 (12 days ago)
|Reward|Rarity|Point amount A|Point amount B|Point amount C|Point amount D|Point amount E|Total amount; possible per round|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[White Gift]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|3|3|1|0|0|7|
|[[Green Gift]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|0|0|2|2|2|6|
|[[Red Gift]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|0|0|0|1|1|2|
There are 5 rounds. Therefore, the maximum total amount is
|Reward|Total amount|
|-|-|
|[[White Gift]]|35|
|[[Green Gift]]|30|
|[[Red Gift]]|10|
Players can also earn a [[Volcanic Rock]] for each defended Gift Pile.
## Tips
During the event, it is recommended to use the [[Snow Suit Armor|Snow Suit]], including the equipment part or the upgrade - [[Nutcracker Armor]] as well as a [[Reindeer Pet]], as you can fire your snow weapon and never deplete its ammo. An upgraded version of [[Frosty the Snow Cannon]], the [[Frosty the Snow Blaster]] or [[Frosty the Howitzer|Howitzer]] is also recommended, as it fires at a higher rate, resulting in more damage overall. It's also recommended to use [[Dwarf Turtle Shelmet]] or [[Shellwise Shard]] to prevent [[Knockback]] taken from the fireballs shot by the Magma Cubes. Prioritize the Magma Cubes as you will lower the risk of losing gifts, and more damage to them means more gifts you earn. Players manning the mounted Snow Cannons should prioritize firing on any crowds of Liquid Hot Magma to keep allies from being overwhelmed by them. The Magma Cubes are not damaged by the mounted Snow Cannons.
[[Foxy]] will increase the gifts obtained by 2.5x while she is elected with the Benevolence Perk.
## Trivia
- Even though the event only happens in the Late Winter, the developers wanted to stress test the new patch with improvements to the lag problem and started the event on the December 24th. 2019 (SkyBlock Time: 18 of Late Summer). This was communicated through the pseudonym [System] and later [Jerry].
- The names and [[Health]] of the Magma Cubes spawning in order are: A sprite icon representing Cubie in Hypixel SkyBlock Cubie 100 , [[Maggie]] 200 , A sprite icon representing Cubert in Hypixel SkyBlock Cubert 300 , A sprite icon representing Cübe in Hypixel SkyBlock Cübe 400 , A sprite icon representing Cubette in Hypixel SkyBlock Cubette 500 , A sprite icon representing Magmalene in Hypixel SkyBlock Magmalene 600 , A sprite icon representing Lucky 7 in Hypixel SkyBlock Lucky 7 700 , A sprite icon representing 8ball in Hypixel SkyBlock 8ball 800 , A sprite icon representing Mega Cube in Hypixel SkyBlock Mega Cube 900 and A sprite icon representing Super Cube in Hypixel SkyBlock Super Cube 900 .
- The Defend Jerry Workshop was closed on March 26th due to lag, but then re-opened soon after.
- Super Cube has the same health as Mega Cube.
- Mounted snow cannons will only deal damage to [[Defend Jerry's Workshop|Liquid Hot Magma]], but not Magma Cubes.
- The [[Snow Suit Armor]] and [[Nutcracker Armor]] sets erroneously name this event "Gift Attack".
- [[Gary]], [[Hendrik]], [[Sherry]][Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] and [[St. Jerry]] can not be given a [[Bachelor's Rose]] during [[Jerry's Workshop]] if the Defend Jerry's Workshop event is not active.
## Bugs
- There is currently a major bug where if a wave (usually the last one) begins on Late Winter 27th, the wave will be bugged and never begin. The scoreboard timer will read "Soon!" and never change until Jerry's Workshop closes. This bug was partially fixed. In some lobbies, it is fixed but not in some others. It is recommended to swap lobbies when your lobby is having the bug.
  - The maximum waiting time from the start of Late Winter 24th to the 1st wave which guarantees the 5th wave is approximately real-life 12 minutes. Players can enter a lobby right before Late Winter 24th, then change to another lobby if the waiting time is too long.
- Bats from the [[Witch Mask]] and the [[Vampire Witch Mask]] can kill the cubes in one shot.
- On July 30th 2021, there was a bug in a certain server (mini569J) that caused Liquid Hot Magmas and power-ups to continue to spawn after the event was over. They could be killed with normal [[Weapons]] (and were able to be insta-killed after the first hit with [[Venomous]]), however abilities didn't work. This allowed players to get [[Combat]] XP and [[Coins]] very fast, assuming they had an [[Intimidation Artifact]] so they weren't killed. They clustered at a specific location, likely as a gift pile should have been there. Whenever a player went too high above the Liquid Hot Magma, they are shot at with fire balls (and were killed instantly if they didn't have an [[Intimidation Artifact]]).
- [[Necromancy]] and some [[Pets]], when hit by a Snow Cannon, will add on your damage counter and can allow you to hit a high score really fast.
- There is a bug where the gifts appear but you cannot pick them up.
- Melee hits deals 1 damage to the cubes.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Dec 18, 2019|0.7.5|Added Defend Jerry's Workshop.|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Increased the amount of gifts that drop during the event.|

# Equipment Menu
Categories: Outdated pages, Pages with UIs, Gloves, Necklaces, Belts, Bracelets, Armor, SkyBlock Menu
This article needs to be reworked as its content is outdated. You can help the wiki by editing this page and reworking it.
This article was last edited by Pigicial (contribs|logs|block) on 3:47, July 23 2026 (3 months ago)
[Equipment Menu]
Type: Equipment Storage
Appearance: Player Head
Usage: Storage of Armor Sets, Bracelets, Gloves, Belts and Necklaces
The Equipment Menu is an interface accessed via the [[SkyBlock Menu]], where players can store their [[Armor]], [[Cloaks]], [[Necklaces]], [[Bracelets]], [[Gloves]] and [[Belts]]. It can be unlocked upon reaching [[Combat|Combat V (5)]].
The Menu is accessed by clicking on the Player Head in the [[SkyBlock Menu]]. The interface will display the Player's Stats, will offer a Stat breakdown, and displays what [[Armor]], [[Cloaks|Cloak]], [[Necklaces|Necklace]], [[Bracelets|Bracelet]], [[Gloves|Glove]] and [[Belts|Belt]] the player currently has equipped. Armor pieces can be removed and placed here, similarly to in the [[Wardrobe]], and is the only way for the player to equip Cloaks, Necklaces, Bracelets, Gloves and Belts. The Equipment Menu also displays the Player's active [[Pets|Pet]].
## Trivia
- When [[Equipment]] was first being tested on the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]], [[Armor]], [[Cloaks]], [[Necklaces]], [[Bracelets]], [[Gloves]] and [[Belts]] could only be equipped in the [[Wardrobe]], meaning that the Equipment Menu's only use was to view the Player's active Equipment, Pet and Stats.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Dec 14, 2021|Added Equipment Menu.|
|Sep 2, 2022|AHN 0.15|Now contains SkyBlock Experience ([[SkyBlock Levels]]) rewards.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Added Equipment Menu.|
|Aug 22, 2023|0.19.3|The stats breakdown menu will now let the player see why they have 0 of a stat rather than saying, "You have none of this stat!".|
Linked from: [[Achievements]], [[Belts]], [[Boots]], [[Bracelets]], [[Chestplates]], [[Cloaks]], [[Equipment]], [[Equipment Wardrobe]], [[Gloves]], [[Helmets]], [[Necklaces]], [[SkyBlock Menu]]

# Farming Fortune

[Farming Fortune]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increases the amount of drops from crops and pests.
Increasing: [[Farming]], [[Hoes]], Farming Axes ([[Axes]]), Armor, Reforges, [[Pets]] and more
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E051
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Player Skull ()
Farming Fortune is a stat that affects a player's chance to get more crops. It has no effect while on one's [[Private Island]].
## Increasing Base Farming Fortune
- [[Farming]] - +4 per level - up to +240 with [[Farming|Farming LX (60)]]
- [[Anita|Extra Farming Fortune]] perk - +4 with each level - up to +60 with 15 tiers
- [[Account & Profile Upgrades|Garden Farming Fortune Account Upgrade]] - +4 per level - up to +40 (only applied while on [[The Garden]]).
- [[Crop Analyzer|Crop Analyzer Milestones]] - +5 with each level - up to +30
- [[The Garden]]
  - [[The Garden|Crop Upgrades]] - +5 [[Crop Fortune]] with each level - up to +45 [[Crop Fortune]], towards a specific crop.
  - [[The Garden|Garden Plot Land]] - +3 for each plot of land owned - up to +72 with all plots owned
  - [[Pests|Garden Bestiary]] - up to +100 with 15 [[Pests|Pest]] [[Bestiary]]

## Scaling
Crop chances are additive; to calculate the crop chance, add the percentage of every bonus that the player has. For example, if the player had a farming level of 20, they would have a 80% chance. The first digit (for 3-digit) or first two digits (for 4-digit) in someone's farming fortune is how many extra drops they are guaranteed to get. The following digits are the chance of getting 1 more drop. For example, if someone had a farming fortune of 260, they would get 3x drops every time and a 60% chance to get 4x drops.
## Theoretical Maximum
The theoretical maximum can be achieved while farming in the [[The Garden|Garden]] with the following:
### Permanent & Unchangeable sources
- [[Farming|Farming LX (60)]] (+240 )
- [[Anita|Extra Farming Fortune]] perk from [[Anita]] (+60 )
- 24 Garden Plots unlocked (+72 )
- Community Shop [[Account & Profile Upgrades|Garden Farming Fortune]] upgrade at [[Elizabeth]] (+40 )
- Garden Bestiary (+100 )
- Consuming five [[Rosewater Flask]] (+5 )
- DNA Analysis Milestone VI from [[Crop Analyzer|Jake's Crop Analyzer]] (+30 )
- [[Anita|Personal Best]] from [[Anita]] (+100 [[Crop Fortune]])
- [[The Desk/UI|Garden Crop]] upgrades (+45 [[Crop Fortune]])
#### Carrolyn
- [[Wheat]]: 3,000 (46 stacks plus 56) [[Fine Flour]] (+12 [[Crop Fortune|Wheat Fortune]])
- [[Carrot]]: 3,000 [[Exportable Carrots]] (+12 [[Crop Fortune|Carrot Fortune]])
- [[Pumpkin]]: 3,000 [[Expired Pumpkin]] (+12 [[Crop Fortune|Pumpkin Fortune]])
- [[Cocoa Beans]]: 3,000 [[Supreme Chocolate Bar]] (+12 [[Crop Fortune|Cocoa Beans Fortune]])
- [[Cactus]]: 3,000 [[Potted Cactus]] (+12 [[Crop Fortune|Cactus Fortune]])
- [[Mushroom]]: 3,000 [[Half-Eaten Mushroom]] (+12 [[Crop Fortune|Mushroom Fortune]])
- [[Nether Wart]]: 3,000 [[Warty]] (+12 [[Crop Fortune|Nether Wart Fortune]])
- [[Wild Rose]]: 3,000 [[Prickly Kiss]] (+12 [[Crop Fortune|Wild Rose Fortune]])
#### Miscellaneous
- [[Chocolate Factory|Chocolate Fortune]] perk (+25 [[Crop Fortune|Cocoa Beans Fortune]])
- Consuming five [[Dark Cacao Truffle|Refined Dark Cacao Truffle]] (+5 [[Crop Fortune|Cocoa Beans Fortune]])
This comes out to +547 Farming Fortune, +145 [[Crop Fortune]] towards any crop, +157 [[Crop Fortune]] towards any crop in Carrolyn's Exportable Crops list, and +187 [[Crop Fortune|Cocoa Beans Fortune]].
### Permanent & Changeable sources
#### Pet
- [Lv200] [[Rose Dragon Pet]] (+40 )
  - All 10 maxed unique [[Pets|Farming Pets]] (+30 )
  - [[Farming|Farming LX (60)]] (+180 )
  - All [[The Garden|Crop Milestones]] maxed (+89.7 )
  - Holding a [[Green Bandana]] while [[The Garden|Garden XV (15)]] (+60 )
#### Armor & Equipment
- Wearing a full set of [[Helianthus Armor]] (+225 )
  - Recombobulated and reforged to [[Reforging|Mossy]] (+120 )
  - [[Gemstone|Perfect Peridot Gemstone]] in all 8 [[Gemstone Slot|slots]] (+80 )
  - [[Pesterminator|Pesterminator VI]] on all pieces of armor (+48 )
- Wearing the full [[Blossom Set]] with 2,500 [[Garden Visitors|Visitors]] served (+118 )
  - All pieces recombobulated and reforged to [[Reforging|Rooted]] (+72 )
  - [[Green Thumb (Enchantment)|Green Thumb V]] with 140 unique visitors served (+140 )
#### Talismans, Attributes & Garden Chips
- [[Farming Talisman]] (+3 )
- [[Helianthus Relic]] (+40 )
- A recombobulated [[Relic of Power]] with all Perfect Gemstones slotted within it, including a [[Gemstone|Perfect Peridot Gemstone]] (+5 )
- [[Attributes|Fortunate Farmer X]] ( [[Fly Shard]]) (+25 )
- [[Attributes|Lunar Power X]] ( [[Lunar Moth Shard]]) or [[Attributes|Solar Power X]] ( [[Firefly Shard]]) (+50 )
- [[Attributes|Ultimate DNA X]] ( [[Galaxy Fish Shard]]) (+10 )
- [[Garden Chips|Cropshot Chip]] (+100 )
#### Farming Tool
- Level 50 Mk. III [[Farming Tools|Specialized Farming Tool]](+200 [[Crop Fortune]])
  - Recombobulated and reforged to [[Reforging|Overpriced]] or [[Reforging|Earthy]] (+25 )
  - [[Gemstone|Perfect Peridot Gemstone]] in all 4 [[Gemstone Slot|slots]] (+32 )
  - [[Harvesting|Harvesting VI]] (+75 )
  - [[Cultivating|Cultivating X]] (+20 )
  - 5 [[Farming for Dummies]] (+5 )
  - [[Turbo-Crop|Turbo-Crop VII]] (+35 [[Crop Fortune]])
  - [[Dedication|Dedication IV]] with a max crop milestone (+92 [[Crop Fortune]])
The new max now comes out to +2139.7 Farming Fortune, +472 [[Crop Fortune]] towards any crop, +484 [[Crop Fortune]] towards any crop in Carrolyn's Exportable Crops list, and +514 [[Crop Fortune|Cocoa Beans Fortune]].
### Temporary Sources
All temporary Farming Fortune sources below are affected by the [[Garden Chips|Hypercharge Chip]]. With [[Garden Chips|Hypercharge]] at level 20, it will double all of these sources:
- [[Century Cakes|Chocolate Century Cake]] (+5 )
- [[Celestial Mason Jar]] (+15 )
- [[Pesthunter Phillip]] pest turn-in (+200 )
- [[Atmospheric Filter]] (+25 )
  - This can be buffed by the [[Attributes|Filter Upgrade X]] ( [[Mite Shard]]) by +20% (+5 )[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
    - Only during Spring.
- [[Magic 8 Ball]] (+25 )
  - Only for one season chosen at random.
- [[Crop Fever|Crop Fever I–V]] enchantment (+100 )
With a max level [[Garden Chips|Hypercharge Chip]], the temporary Farming Fortune comes out to +750 Farming Fortune.
There are more temporary sources of Farming Fortune that are not affected by the [[Garden Chips|Hypercharge Chip]], which are:
- Equipping [[Zorro's Cape]] rather than [[Blossom Cloak]], as it yields more Farming Fortune during a Jacob's Contest due to its stats being doubled.
  - [[Reforging|Rooted]] reforge on [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] (+21 )
  - [[Green Thumb (Enchantment)|Green Thumb V]] enchantment with 140 unique visitors served (+35 )
    - During a farming contest, the Zorro's Cape grants +132 Farming Fortune, outperforming the [[Blossom Cloak]] by +49.5 Farming Fortune
- [[Attributes|Infiltration X]] ( [[Earthworm Shard]]) (+30 only when standing inside of a plot with 1 or more [[Pest (Mob Type)|Pest]] within it)
- [[Harvest Harbinger Potion]] (+50 )
- [[Doug|Fortunate Feasting]] perk while a [[Harvest Feast]] or [[Harvest Feast|Grand Feast]] is active (+25 )
  - Since the [[Atmospheric Filter]] requires the Spring season to grant Farming Fortune, and Harvest Feast only being active during Autumn, it is required to have a [[Finnegan|Finnegan Mayor]] active with the Grand Feast perk active to achieve both at the same time.
- [[Melon Juice Mixin]] (+15 )
- [[Anita's Artifact]] (+25 [[Crop Fortune]] towards 1 of the 3 specific crops during a [[Jacob's Farming Contest|Jacob's Contest]])
- A level 20 [[Garden Chips|Overdrive Chip]] (+140 [[Crop Fortune]] towards all 3 specific crops during a Jacob's Contest)
- [[Dark Cacao Truffle|Refined Dark Cacao Truffle]] (+30 [[Crop Fortune|Cocoa Beans Fortune]])
With a max level [[Garden Chips|Hypercharge Chip]], the temporary Farming Fortune now comes out to +919.5 Farming Fortune, +165 [[Crop Fortune]], and +195 [[Crop Fortune|Cocoa Beans Fortune]].
### Summary[Confirm]
The maximum theoretically achievable Farming Fortune is:
- +3059.2 Farming Fortune, +637 [[Crop Fortune]], +649 [[Crop Fortune]] towards any crop in Carrolyn's Exportable Crops list, and +709 [[Crop Fortune|Cocoa Beans Fortune]].
  - This yields any crop to have +3696.2 Farming Fortune, granting 36 bonus drops and a 96.2% chance to drop another.
  - This yields any crop that's in Carrolyn's Exportable Crops list to have +3708.2 Farming Fortune, granting 37 bonus drops and a 8.2% chance to drop another.
  - This yields Cocoa Beans to have +3768.2 Farming Fortune, granting 37 bonus drops and a 68.2% chance to drop another.

# Four-Eyed Fish
Categories: Outdated pages, Epic items, Items, Craftable items, Pet items, Pets
This article needs to be reworked as its content is outdated.
You can help the wiki by editing this page and reworking it.
Reason: Need to add interactions on burrows 2 and 9 and some "maf" for CoA
This article was last edited by Pigicial's Pet Robot (contribs|logs|block) on 9:45, September 6 2026 (50 days ago)
[Four-Eyed Fish]
Type: Pet Item ([[Pet Items]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FOUR_EYED_FISH
Tooltip: Consumed on use; This pet item may only be used on a Griffin pet.; Gain +2,000 coins when digging up any Griffin Burrow.; Grants +13 Magic Find and +55 Defense.; Right-click on your summoned pet to give it this item!; EPIC PET ITEM
Four-Eyed Fish is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]]. It grants bonus stats to the [[Griffin Pet]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Lucky Clover]] 1x [[Pre-Digestion Fish]] 1x [[Reinforced Scales]] 384x (6 stacks (384 items total)) [[Enchanted Spider Eye]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Spider Eye]] x64 ×6 + [[Lucky Clover]] + [[Pre-Digestion Fish]] + [[Reinforced Scales]] → Four-Eyed Fish|
## Usage
Right-clicking a [[Griffin Pet]] whilst holding a Four-Eyed Fish will equip it to the [[Pets|Pet]], granting it +13 [[Magic Find]] and +55 [[Defense]], as well as a 2,000 [[Coins]] reward every time the player digs [[Griffin Burrows]]. If another [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] were equipped on the [[Pets|Pet]], it would refund the Four-Eyed Fish for 250,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.
## Trivia
- Four-eyed fishes do exist in the real world, in the genus Anableps.
- The Four-Eyed Fish could be a reference to Blinky the Three-Eyed Fish from The Simpsons.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 4, 2023|Added Four-Eyed Fish.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Four-Eyed Fish.|
|May 5, 2026|Increased [[Magic Find]] granted from +10 to +13 .|
